- `__glOdyim_IsEnabled` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd956830, 680 bytes): **established.**
  Answers glIsEnabled for the enumerants whose state only the board half keeps (argument: the enumerant; result 0 or 1). GL_RESCALE_NORMAL tests +0x704 bit 16; GL_FOG_OFFSET_SGIX bit 4; GL_PIXEL_TEXTURE_SGIS bit 14; GL_FRAGMENT_LIGHTING_SGIX bit 12 and GL_FRAGMENT_COLOR_MATERIAL_SGIX bit 13 of the same word; GL_ASYNC_HISTOGRAM_SGIX tests +0x718 bit 3 and the three other async enumerants its bits 0 to 2; GL_VERTEX_PRECLIP_SGIX returns the byte at +0x19db4; the eight fragment lights (0x840c to 0x8413) test bit n of +0x1647c. Any other enumerant tail-calls the generic `__glim_IsEnabled`. Inside glBegin and glEnd it raises 0x502 and returns 0.

## Attribute stack

glPushAttrib and glPopAttrib are kept in two parallel pointer stacks: the hardware-state records (stack array at +0x168d0, top pointer at +0x168d4, depth limit at +0xec0 entries) and the generic-state records (base at +0x31b8, top at +0x31bc). A push fills a pair of records, a 0xed50-byte one for the board's state and a 0xbd0-byte one for the generic GL state, both allocated with the context's calloc entry (+4) the first time a stack slot is used; the first word of the generic record keeps the attribute mask. In what follows "rec1" is the 0xed50-byte record and "rec2" the 0xbd0-byte one. The mask bits are the public GL attribute bits: 0x1 current, 0x2 point, 0x4 line, 0x8 polygon, 0x10 polygon stipple, 0x20 pixel mode, 0x40 lighting, 0x80 fog, 0x100 depth buffer, 0x200 accumulation buffer, 0x400 stencil buffer, 0x800 viewport, 0x1000 transform, 0x2000 enable, 0x4000 colour buffer, 0x8000 hint, 0x10000 evaluator, 0x20000 list, 0x40000 texture, 0x80000 scissor.

- `PushAttributes` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd957f00, 3772 bytes): **established.**
  Saves the state a mask selects (arguments: the mask, a pointer to the slot's rec1 pointer, a pointer to the slot's rec2 pointer). Inside glBegin and glEnd (global 0x1018 equal to 1) it raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION and returns. It allocates both records when the slot's rec1 pointer is 0 and stores the mask in rec2's first word. It always copies the enable words +0x700 to +0x71c to rec2+0x650, the board's raster-control and second control words +0x7bfc and +0x7c00 to rec1+0x84 and rec1+0x88, and +0x1647c (the fragment-light enables) to rec1+0xe904. Per mask bit: 0x200 the four accumulation clear values +0x670 to +0x67c to rec2+0x5c0; 0x4000 the word +0x71c and 18 words from +0x720 to rec2+0x66c, and the board words +0x7c28, +0x7c34, +0x7c38, +0x7c3c, +0x7c40 and +0x7c5c to rec1+0xb0 to rec1+0xe4; 0x1 the 272 bytes of current-vertex state from +0xb8 to rec2+8, then the current colour (0xb00), index (0xb01), normal (0xb02), texture coordinate (0xb03), raster index (0xb05; the raster colour 0xb04 instead when the byte at +0xc94 is nonzero), raster texture coordinate (0xb06) and edge flag (0xb43), read through the get entries at +0x1f0 and +0x1e0 of the context's dispatch table; 0x100 the doubles +0x660 and +0x668 and the board words +0x7c30 and +0x7c5c; 0x80 ten fog words from +0x634 and the board words +0x7c08 and +0x7c0c, and the 76-byte fog table at +0x16430 to rec1+0xe8b8; 0x8000 the 20 bytes of hints at +0x768; 0x40 the 200 bytes of lighting state from +0x568 to +0x62f, a copy of the light array (the count at +0xe04 times 0x74 bytes, newly allocated, from the pointer at +0x630), the 312 bytes of fragment-light state from +0x1647c, and the board words +0x7c70 to +0x7cac, +0x957c to +0x9584 and +0x7c44 to +0x7c54 (when colour material, bit 7 of +0x700, is on it first calls the table entries at +0x130 and +0x134 with GL_COLOR_MATERIAL, **inferred** to be disable and enable so the material state is consistent when copied); 0x2 the three point words +0x1c8 to +0x1d0 and the board word +0x7c58; 0x4 the line words +0x1d4 to +0x1e3 and the board words +0x7c44 to +0x7c4c and +0x7c54; 0x8 the seven polygon words +0x1e4 to +0x1fc and the board words +0x7c04 and +0x7c50; 0x10 the 32 stipple words +0x200 to +0x27f and the 0x80 bytes at +0x7b78 (the board's copy of the pattern); 0x20 the pixel-store and transfer words from +0x280, +0x4d4 and +0x4d8, the colour tables through `__glCopyColorTableScaleBias` (from +0x1538) and the convolution state through `__glCopyConvolutionPixelModeState` (from +0x280), and the board word +0x7c58; **inferred** to be the detail-function points). The enable word +0x700 is stored a second time at the end of the texture block. `scripts/fifo_words.py` over 0xd957f00 to 0xd958dbc lists no FIFO store.
- `PopAttributes` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd958f60, 7472 bytes): **established.**
  Read in full: the decompilation next to the assembly for control flow, FIFO words and calls, and every group's word copies compared with `PushAttributes` by running the push and then this routine in an emulator (`scripts/attrib_roundtrip.py`) on a context whose every word held its own offset, one mask bit at a time. In that run each group came back to the same offsets it was saved from (listed below) and no group wrote another group's words. Restores the state a push saved (arguments: rec1 and rec2, in the order the push produced them) and makes the board see it. It raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION inside glBegin and glEnd, takes the mask from rec2's first word and undoes each selected group. Enable (0x2000): when the saved word has the scissor-test bit (bit 14 of +0x700) clear and the live one set it calls `__glReadjustZCounts`, and when it is the other way round it copies +0x7a84 to +0x7a88; it restores +0x957c to +0x9584 and +0x700 to +0x71c and +0x1647c, merges the saved board words into +0x7bfc (saved word masked with 0x5fe1df) and +0x7c00 (saved word masked with 0xe000), sets +0x16960 bits 1 and 2, +0x16964 0xfc0 and +0x2e24 0x3e, calls the context's scissor entries at +0x2ec4 and +0x2ebc and `ValidateColorMaterial`, and sets +0x7cd4 bit 0 from bit 31 of +0x700. Accumulation (0x200): the four clear values at +0x670 to +0x67c. Colour buffer (0x4000): the blend, logic and mask words; it writes +0x7c28 and sends `0x1041e` with it, restores +0x7c34 to +0x7c3c (marking +0x16960 bit 0 so the validator resends them), writes +0x7c40 and sends `0x10422` with it, merges bits 20 to 23 and 11:0 of +0x7c5c, and calls the table entry at +0x100 with the word at +0x760. Depth (0x100): the doubles, +0x7c30 sent as `0x10420`, bit 24 of +0x7c5c. Evaluator (0x10000): twelve words at +0x78c to +0x7bb and +0x710 and +0x714. Fog (0x80): ten words, +0x7c08 and +0x7c0c, the fog table, and +0x16964 bit 12. Hint (0x8000): the 20 bytes at +0x768 to +0x77b, with no board word. Lighting (0x40): +0x708, the 200 bytes, the light array by memcpy (then released), the light dirty masks +0x16968 and +0x1696c set for every light, the fragment-light words, the board light words, then `ValidateColorMaterial` and a replay of the saved front (0x404) and back (0x405) material values (ambient 0x1200, diffuse 0x1201, specular 0x1202, emission 0x1600, shininess 0x1601, colour indexes 0x1603) through the table entries at +0x80 and +0x7c. Current (0x1): the 272 bytes back to +0xb8, the byte +0xa73c cleared, and the table entries at +0x5dc (with +0x1b0), +0x610 (with the word at +0x1c0), +0x644 (with +0xd0), +0x6c4 (with +0xe0) and +0x24 (with the byte at +0x1c5) called. Line (0x4): the words back and `0x10c48` with +0x7c44, +0x7c48 and +0x7c4c after the settle prefix when needed. Polygon (0x8): seven words, `0x10415` with +0x7c04 and `0x10458` with +0x7c50. Polygon stipple (0x10): the 0x80 bytes back to +0x7b78 and the three stipple blocks `0x1043f` (with +0x7bf8), `0x13c4e` and `0x13c4f` (16 words each). Scissor (0x80000): the scissor words, with a `__glReadjustZCounts` when they differ. Stencil (0x400): the words back, +0x7c2c, bits 19:12 of +0x7c5c, +0x16960 bits 5 and 7 and +0x16964 bit 11, no FIFO store. Texture (0x40000): the texgen records and bytes, the environment through `__glOdyim_TexEnviv` and `__glOdyim_TexEnvfv` (GL_TEXTURE_ENV_MODE and GL_TEXTURE_ENV_COLOR), and for each of the four targets (`TargetIndexToTargetEnum`) `__glOdyim_BindTexture` of the saved texture and `__glOdyim_TexParameteriv` and `__glOdyim_TexParameterfv` for GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, WRAP_T, WRAP_R, MIN_FILTER, MAG_FILTER, GL_TEXTURE_BORDER_COLOR, GL_TEXTURE_PRIORITY, GL_DETAIL_TEXTURE_LEVEL_SGIS, GL_DETAIL_TEXTURE_MODE_SGIS, GL_POST_TEXTURE_FILTER_BIAS_SGIX and SCALE_SGIX, minimum and maximum LOD, base and maximum level, the three GL_TEXTURE_LOD_BIAS_*_SGIX and the three GL_TEXTURE_MAX_CLAMP_*_SGIX values, and for GL_TEXTURE_2D `__glOdyim_DetailTexFuncSGIS`. - `__glOdyim_PushAttrib` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd958dc0, 212 bytes): **established.**
  glPushAttrib (argument: the mask). Raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION (0x502) inside glBegin and glEnd; when the hardware stack's top pointer (+0x168d4) is not below the base (+0x168d0) plus four times the depth limit (+0xec0) it raises GL_STACK_OVERFLOW (0x503). Otherwise it calls `PushAttributes` with the mask and the two stack tops, then adds 4 to each top (+0x168d4 and +0x31bc).
- `__glOdyim_PopAttrib` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95ad10, 160 bytes): **established.**
  glPopAttrib. Raises 0x502 inside glBegin and glEnd and GL_STACK_UNDERFLOW (0x504) when the hardware stack's top is not above its base; otherwise it takes the two record pointers just below the two tops, lowers both tops by 4 and calls `PopAttributes` with them.
- `__glOdysseyFreeAttributeState` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95adb0, 232 bytes): **established.**
  Throws away a context's attribute stack when it is destroyed (argument: the context). It pops until the generic stack is empty (calling `__glOdyim_PopAttrib` each time, which also applies the popped state), frees every non-null record pointer in the hardware stack array up to the first null, then the array itself, with the context's release entry at +0xc, and zeroes +0x168d0.
- `TargetIndexToTargetEnum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd958f10, 76 bytes): **established.**
  Maps the saved-texture slot number to the texture target: 0 gives GL_TEXTURE_1D (0xde0), 1 GL_TEXTURE_2D (0xde1), 2 GL_TEXTURE_3D (0x806f), 3 GL_DETAIL_TEXTURE_2D_SGIS (0x8095); anything else gives 0.
- `__glOdyssey_CopyContext` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd958ea0, 112 bytes): **established.**
  Saves the current context's state into the records of another context (arguments: a0, a1, a2). It loads the current-context word at absolute address 4112 and compares it with a1; when they differ it returns 1 and does nothing else. When they are equal it calls `PushAttributes` (0xd957f00, reached by `bgezall` at 0xd958ef4) with a2 as its first argument, the mask, and the addresses a0 + 0x168c8 and a0 + 0x168cc as its second and third, the pointers to the two record slots that `PushAttributes` allocates on first use, and returns 1. So a0 is the context whose two slots receive the saved records, a1 is the context that must be the current one, and a2 is the attribute mask; the frame is 16 bytes, saving gp and ra.
- `__glOdyssey_ResolveContext` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95ac90, 128 bytes): **established.**
  The destination side of the copy: it applies the two scratch records at +0x168c8 and +0x168cc to the current context with `PopAttributes`, releases both with the context's release entry, and zeroes the two pointers.

## Evaluators

The evaluator is done on the host: the routines below evaluate the enabled maps at a parameter value and hand the results to the context's own current-value entry points, which put them on the board like ordinary vertex calls. State used by all of them: the map enables at +0x710 (one-dimensional maps) and +0x714 (two-dimensional maps), where bit 0 is the colour map, 1 the index map, 2 the normal map, 3 to 6 the texture-coordinate maps of 1 to 4 components, 7 the 3-component vertex map and 8 the 4-component vertex map; the map descriptors at +0x6e84 (nine 0x18-byte records of the one-dimensional maps) and +0x6f5c (nine 0x28-byte records of the two-dimensional ones), each record starting with the point size in floats, then the order and the two parameter limits; the control-point pointers in two separate arrays, at +0x70c4 (nine for the one-dimensional maps) and +0x70e8 (nine for the two-dimensional ones), in the map order of the enable bits; the evaluation grid words, +0x78c to +0x790 and +0x798 (one-dimensional grid: start, end, divisions) and +0x79c to +0x7b8 (two-dimensional: u start, end, divisions, v start, end, divisions); and the context's own entry-point table at +0x17f4, in which the slots +0x5dc, +0x614, +0x644, +0x6c4, +0x554 and +0x574 take a colour, an index, a normal, a texture coordinate and a 3- or 4-component vertex (**inferred** from the arguments each call passes and from the map each follows). An evaluation writes its results to a 17-word record: word 0 is a mask of the values present (1 colour, 2 index, 4 normal, 8 texture coordinate, 0x10 3-component vertex, 0x20 4-component vertex), words 1 to 4 the colour (or the index in word 1), words 5 to 7 the normal, words 9 to 12 the texture coordinate and words 13 to 16 the vertex.

- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1d` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976520, 56 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord1d (argument: a double). It narrows the double to a float and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` with it and the context from the process address 0x1010; nothing else is done.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1dv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976560, 60 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord1dv (argument: a pointer to a double). It loads the double, narrows it to a float and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` with the context from the process address 0x1010.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1f` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9765a0, 56 bytes): **established.**

- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1fv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9765e0, 60 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord1fv (argument: a pointer to a float). It loads the float and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` with it and the context from the process address 0x1010.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2d` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976620, 64 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord2d (arguments: two doubles). It narrows both to floats and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` with them and the context from the process address 0x1010.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2dv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976660, 68 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord2dv (argument: a pointer to two doubles). It loads both, narrows them to floats and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` with the context from the process address 0x1010.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2f` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9766b0, 60 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalCoord2f (arguments: two floats). It passes them unchanged to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` with the context from the process address 0x1010.

  glEvalCoord2fv (argument: a pointer to two floats). It loads both and calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` with the context from the process address 0x1010.
- `__glOdyim_EvalPoint1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976800, 112 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalPoint1 (argument: the grid index i). The parameter is the grid end at +0x790 when i equals the division count at +0x798, otherwise i x ((end minus start at +0x78c) divided by the count) plus the start, and `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` evaluates it.
- `__glOdyim_EvalPoint2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976990, 164 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalPoint2 (arguments: i, j). u and v are formed from the two grids (+0x79c, +0x7a0, +0x7a8 and +0x7ac, +0x7b0, +0x7b8) as the one-dimensional point is, each taking the grid's end exactly at its last index, and `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` evaluates them.
- `__glOdyim_EvalMesh1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976730, 208 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalMesh1 (arguments: mode, first index, last index). If the global at 0x1018 is nonzero and not 2 it raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION (0x502); when it is 2 it validates through the context entry at +0x2e40 and clears the global. GL_LINE (0x1b01) calls `__glOdysseyEvalMesh1Line`, GL_POINT (0x1b00) `__glOdysseyEvalMesh1Point`; any other mode raises GL_INVALID_ENUM.
- `__glOdyim_EvalMesh2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976870, 280 bytes): **established.**
  glEvalMesh2 (mode and the index ranges), with the same validation as the one-dimensional routine, and GL_FILL (0x1b02), GL_LINE and GL_POINT dispatched to `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Fill`, `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Line` and `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Point`; the u range and v range indices are passed in the order the internal routines take them (first u, last u, first v, last v).
- `PreEvaluate` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976a40, 400 bytes): **established.**
  Fills the Bernstein basis of the given order at a parameter t (arguments: t, order, output array of order floats). Order 1 gives {1}, 2 gives {1 - t, t}, 3 gives {(1 - t)^2, 2 t (1 - t), t^2} and 4 gives {(1 - t)^3, 3 t (1 - t)^2, 3 t^2 (1 - t), t^3}, each written out; higher orders start from {1 - t, t} and multiply in one more factor (1 - t) and t per step, accumulating in place.
- `PreEvaluateWithDeriv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976be0, 852 bytes): **established.**
  The same basis plus its derivative divided by the degree (arguments: t, order, basis array, derivative array). Order 1 gives derivative {0}; order 2 gives {-1, 1}; order 3 gives {t - 1, (1 - t) - t, t}; order 4 gives {-(1 - t)^2, (1 - t)^2 - 2 t (1 - t), 2 t (1 - t) - t^2, t^2}; higher orders build the basis as in `PreEvaluate` for order - 1 and form the derivative from differences of neighbouring entries of that lower basis, then raise the basis to the full order.
- `DoDomain1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976f40, 1444 bytes): **established.**
  Evaluates one one-dimensional map at u (arguments: u, a work area, the map record, the output array, the control points). The work area is the stack copy of the descriptor block that `DoEvalCoord1` and `DoEvalCoord2` make; the routine keeps a basis cache in it. The parameter is t = (u - u1) / (u2 - u1) from the record's limits, and nothing happens when the limits are equal. When t or the order differs from the cached pair at +0x2f4 and +0x57c of the work area it recomputes the basis with `PreEvaluate` into +0x2fc of the work area, stores the order and t and sets the word at +0x584 to 2. Then every output component i is the sum over the order of basis k times control point k's component i, the control points lying one point after another with the record's first word as the point size; orders 1 to 4 are written out and larger orders loop.
- `DoDomain2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977d80, 8168 bytes): **established.**
  Evaluates one two-dimensional map at (u, v) (arguments: the work area, u and v as floats, the map record, the output array in a4, the control points). The record holds the point size at +0, the u order at +4, the v order at +8 and the limits u1, u2, v1, v2 at +0xc to +0x18; when u1 equals u2 or v1 equals v2 the routine returns without writing anything. The parameters become t_u = (u - u1) / (u2 - u1) and t_v likewise. Each is compared with the work area's cached parameter (+0x2f4 for u, +0x2f8 for v) and the record's order with the cached order (+0x57c, +0x580); a mismatch calls `PreEvaluate` for that axis only, with the basis written to +0x2fc (u) or +0x39c (v), stores the parameter and order and sets the flag at +0x584 or +0x588 to 2. Output component c is the sum over u index j and v index k of the u basis j times the v basis k times control point (j x v order + k) component c, the points lying one after another with the record's first word as the point size. The body is a dispatch on the u order (4, 3, 2, 1, anything else) and inside each on the v order (the same five), and every one of the 25 bodies is unrolled with the loop over components peeled for an odd point size; the orders 5 and above use a loop of multiply-adds. `scripts/evaluator_check.py` runs the routine in an emulator with the basis pre-filled and compares all 49 pairs of orders 1 to 7 at point sizes 1 to 5 with that sum: every one agrees to single-precision rounding, and the stale-cache runs call `PreEvaluate` with the order, the parameter and the basis address above. The emulator was given stand-ins for the multiply-add and conditional-move instructions the routine uses.
- `DoDomain2WithDerivs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd979d70, 10396 bytes): **established** for the argument layout, the cache and every pair of orders except those named near the end, **inferred** for the pairs named there.
  The same evaluation of one two-dimensional map that also gives the two partial derivatives, used for automatic normals (arguments: the work area, u and v, the map record, then the output array in a4, the u-derivative array in a5, the v-derivative array in a6 and the control points in a7; the record, limits and early return are those of `DoDomain2`). The basis cache is the same, except that a recompute calls `PreEvaluateWithDeriv`, which fills the basis (+0x2fc for u, +0x39c for v) and its derivative divided by the degree (+0x43c for u, +0x4dc for v), and stores 1 in the flag at +0x584 or +0x588; a cached basis with the plain routine's flag 2 is therefore recomputed even when t and the order match. The outputs are the sum of u basis j times v basis k times the control point for the position, the sum with the u derivative in place of the u basis for the u-derivative array, and the sum with the v derivative for the v-derivative array. The dispatch is the plain routine's 25 unrolled bodies plus loops, with all three sums formed together. `scripts/evaluator_check.py` ran it in an emulator over orders 1 to 7 and point sizes 1 to 4 (the plain routine's stand-ins, with the Bernstein basis and derivative pre-filled): all three outputs equal the sums except for the u orders 2 to 4 with a v order of 5 or more and the pair u order 2, v order 1. For u order 2 with v order 1 the emulated position output is 1.5 times the first control point (t_u 0.25: the first term, 0.75 x the point, counted twice) and the u derivative is minus two times it: the second accumulation block of the peeled component at 0xd97c2dc to 0xd97c2f4 reloads the control point at 0(ra) and the u basis word at +0x2fc, the same two loads as the first block at 0xd97c298 and 0xd97c29c, so the second control point is never read. For u orders 2 to 4 with v orders 5 or more the emulated run gives zero weight to every u row but the last, in all three outputs (the loop entered at 0xd97ac08). Both findings rest on the emulated run and on the assembly named, and are **inferred** because the emulator stands in for the multiply-add and conditional-move instructions. Whether the library itself returns these values for those orders is **unknown**; running the same blocks under an emulator that implements those instructions would settle it.
- `GetCurrentState` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9774f0, 652 bytes): **established.**
  Saves the current values an evaluation will overwrite (arguments: the context, the dimension 1 or 2, a record). It picks the enable word (+0x710 for 1, +0x714 for 2); the mask in the record's word 0 gets 1 (colour) when the byte at +0xc94 is set and the colour map is enabled, 2 (index) in the index mode of the byte at +0xc95 when the index map is enabled, 4 (normal) for the normal map and 8 (texture coordinate) when any of the four texture maps (bits 3 to 6) is enabled. The values come from the context (colour +0x1b0 to +0x1bc, index +0x1c0, normal +0xd0 to +0xd8, texture coordinate +0xe0 to +0xec) when the table entry at +0x1f0 of the dispatch table is the software `__glOdyim_SoftGetFloatv`; otherwise it asks the hardware shadow with `__glOdyGetFromHardware` (a negative result makes the mask 0) and decodes them from the shadow block at the pointer +0x16d24 (index at +0, colour at +0x14, normal at +0x28, texture coordinate at +0x50) with `__glOdyDecodeShadowFormat`.
- `RestoreCurrentState` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977780, 172 bytes): **established.**
  Puts the values `GetCurrentState` saved back by calling the entries +0x5dc (colour) or +0x614 (index), +0x6c4 (texture coordinate) and +0x644 (normal) for the bits that are set.
- `DoEvalCoord1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977830, 948 bytes): **established.**
  Evaluates every enabled one-dimensional map at u and emits the results (arguments: u, the context). It first copies the 0x58c bytes of map descriptors from +0x6e84 onto the stack. For the colour map (RGBA mode) or the index map (index mode) it calls `DoDomain1` with the map and the control-point pointer from +0x70c4 or +0x70c8 and passes the result to the colour or index entry; for the highest enabled texture map among the four it evaluates 1 to 4 components, zero-fills the missing ones and fills the last component with the context's word at +0xdd8 when fewer than four were evaluated, and calls the texture-coordinate entry; the normal map calls the normal entry; the 4-component vertex map calls the 4-vertex entry (+0x574), and otherwise the 3-component map the 3-vertex entry (+0x554), last.
- `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977bf0, 128 bytes): **established.**
  The common body of the glEvalCoord1 and glEvalPoint1 entries: `GetCurrentState` with dimension 1, `DoEvalCoord1`, `RestoreCurrentState`.
- `ComputeFirstPartials` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977c70, 156 bytes): **established.**
  Converts the partial derivatives of a 4-component vertex map into derivatives of the divided position (arguments: the evaluated point, the two derivative vectors): each derivative's first three components d become w times d minus the derivative's own w times the point's component, with w the point's fourth component.
- `ComputeNormal2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977d10, 100 bytes): **established.**
  Forms a surface normal as the cross product of the two derivative vectors (arguments: the context, the output normal, the two vectors), then calls the context's normalising entry at +0x2eb8 on it.
- `DoEvalCoord2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97c610, 1484 bytes): **established.**
  Evaluates every enabled two-dimensional map at (u, v) into the 17-word record it is given (arguments: u, v, the context, the record). It copies the 0x58c-byte descriptor block to the stack as the one-dimensional routine does and clears the record's mask. When bit 23 of +0x700 (GL_AUTO_NORMAL) is set and a vertex map is enabled, it calls the derivative form of the evaluator (`DoDomain2WithDerivs`) for the 4-component vertex map (bit 8) or the 3-component one (bit 7), calls `ComputeFirstPartials` for the 4-component map, forms the normal with `ComputeNormal2` and sets the mask to normal plus vertex (0x24 or 0x14). Otherwise (with auto-normal off) it evaluates the normal map (bit 2) and then the vertex maps with `DoDomain2`, the 4-component one first, and sets 0x4 and 0x20 or 0x10; with auto-normal on the normal map is not evaluated. Colour (or index, by the visual byte at +0xc95), the highest enabled texture map (with the context's +0xdd8 word for a missing last component and zero for the others) are then evaluated the same way and set bits 1 or 2 and 8. The record's values are finally emitted through the entries +0x5dc or +0x614, +0x6c4, +0x644 and the vertex entry +0x554 (3 components, mask 0x10) or +0x574 (4 components).
- `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97cbe0, 140 bytes): **established.**
  The common body of glEvalCoord2 and glEvalPoint2: `GetCurrentState` with dimension 2, `DoEvalCoord2` with a record on the stack, `RestoreCurrentState`.
- `sendChange` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97ced0, 304 bytes): **established.**
  Replays a stored evaluation record without evaluating again (arguments: the context, the record). It emits the colour (entry +0x5dc), texture coordinate (+0x6c4) and normal (+0x644) whose bits the mask has, then the vertex through +0x554 when bit 0x10 is set or +0x574 when bit 0x20 is. When the colour was replayed and colour material is on (bit 7 of +0x700) it calls the context's entry at +0x2f18, writes the four current-colour words +0x1b0 to +0x1bc back to the values they had on entry and calls the entry again; the entry's role was not read (**inferred**: it applies colour material from the current colour, so the second call restores the material the old colour implies).
- `__glOdysseyEvalMesh1Line` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97cc70, 292 bytes): **established.**
  Draws a one-dimensional mesh as a line strip (arguments: context, first and last grid index). Nothing happens when the division count at +0x798 is 0. It saves the current values, begins primitive 3 (GL_LINE_STRIP) through the table entry at +0x1c, evaluates the grid points from the first index to the last with `DoEvalCoord1` (the end parameter exactly at the last division), ends the primitive through the entry at +0x2c, and restores the current values.
- `__glOdysseyEvalMesh1Point` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97cda0, 292 bytes): **established.**
  Draws a one-dimensional mesh as points (arguments: context, first and last grid index). It returns without drawing when the division count at +0x798 is 0; otherwise it saves the current values with `GetCurrentState`, begins primitive 0 through the table entry at +0x1c, evaluates each grid point from the first index to the last with `DoEvalCoord1` (taking the end parameter at +0x790 exactly at the last division and i x (end - start at +0x78c) / count + start elsewhere), ends the primitive through the entry at +0x2c and restores the current values with `RestoreCurrentState`.
- `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Point` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97d2f0, 476 bytes): **established.**
  Draws a two-dimensional mesh as points (arguments: context, first and last u index, first and last v index). It does nothing when either division count (+0x7a8, +0x7b8) is 0. It saves the current values, begins primitive 0, evaluates every (u, v) grid pair with `DoEvalCoord2` (a grid end taken exactly at its last index), ends the primitive and restores.
- `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Fill` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97d000, 744 bytes): **established.**
  Draws a two-dimensional mesh as filled quad strips (arguments: a0 the context, a1 the lower u index, a2 the lower v index, a3 the end u index, exclusive, a4 the upper v index, inclusive). It returns at once, before touching any state, when the u division count at +0x7a8 (1960) or the v division count at +0x7b8 (1976) is zero. Otherwise it calls `GetCurrentState` (0xd9774f0) with the context, 2 and the record 120 bytes below the frame top; sp is lowered by 0x11180 (lui 1, ori 0x1180, subu) and s8 holds the old sp, then forms du as (+0x7a0 minus +0x79c) over the u count and dv as (+0x7b0 minus +0x7ac) over the v count, both as floats. The outer loop runs i from a1 while i is below a3. Each pass takes u at the low side as +0x79c plus i times du, or +0x7a0 when i equals the u count, and u at the high side the same way for i + 1; it then calls the Begin entry at +28 of the dispatch table at context +0x17f4 (6132) with 8 (quad strip). When a4 is below a2 the inner loop is skipped. Otherwise v steps from a4 down to a2 inclusive, v being +0x7ac plus the index times dv or +0x7b0 when the index equals the v count, and each step sends two vertices, the low-u vertex first and then the high-u vertex, each a call of `DoEvalCoord2` (0xd97c610) with the context, u in f13, v in f14 and a record address in a3. The frame holds 1024 records of 68 bytes (17 words) ending at that saved-state record, and one spare 68-byte record below them (the table starts 69752 and the spare record 69824 bytes below the frame top); the step counter s2 counts from 0 and the record slot advances 68 bytes per step. While the counter is below 1024, the high-u vertex is evaluated into its slot of the table, and the low-u vertex is sent by `sendChange` (0xd97ced0) with that slot as the record when the strip is not the first (i not equal to a1), so it replays the high-u record the previous strip stored for the same step, before the slot is overwritten by the new high-u record; in the first strip the low-u vertex is evaluated into the spare record instead. From step 1024 on both vertices of every strip are evaluated into the spare record, with no replay and nothing kept. After the inner loop it calls the End entry at +44 of the same dispatch table, and the outer loop ends when i reaches a3. At the end it calls `RestoreCurrentState` (0xd977780) with the context and the saved record, and returns; it sets no return value.
- `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Line` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd97d4d0, 1336 bytes): **established.**
  Draws a two-dimensional mesh as lines (arguments: a0 the context, a1 the lower u index, a2 the lower v index, a3 the end u index, exclusive, a4 the upper v index, inclusive). It returns at once when the u division count at +0x7a8 (1960) is zero, and when the v division count at +0x7b8 (1976) is zero, before touching any state. Otherwise it calls `GetCurrentState` (0xd9774f0) with the context, 2 and the record 120 bytes below the frame top, forms du as (+0x7a0 minus +0x79c) over the u count and dv as (+0x7b0 minus +0x7ac) over the v count, and ends by calling `RestoreCurrentState` (0xd977780) with the context and that record; it sets no return value. The frame is lowered by 0x11b0 and holds the same 1024-record table of 68-byte (17-word) records as `__glOdysseyEvalMesh2Fill`, starting 69752 bytes below the frame top, and the same spare record 69824 bytes below it. While a1 is below a3, the outer loop takes u at the low side from the index i (+0x79c plus i times du, or +0x7a0 when i equals the u count) and u at the high side the same way for i + 1. When a4 is not below a2, an inner loop runs j from a2 to a4 inclusive, with v at j and at j + 1 formed the same way from +0x7ac and +0x7b0 (v2 when the index equals the v count). Each j is its own line strip: Begin at +28 of the dispatch table at context +0x17f4 with 3 (line strip), up to three vertices, then End at +44. The vertices are, in order: (low u, v at j + 1), skipped when j equals a4; (low u, v at j); and (high u, v at j). Each is a call of `DoEvalCoord2` (0xd97c610, u in f13, v in f14, a record address in a3) or a replay by `sendChange` (0xd97ced0) of a record. A pass counter s5 starts at 0 in every column and rises by 1 per j. While s5 is 1024 or more, the second and third vertices of the pass are evaluated into the spare record, and the first as well from s5 = 1023 up; nothing is kept. Below that, the third vertex is always evaluated into table record s5, and a first vertex (when it is sent at all) is evaluated into record s5 + 1 in the first column and replayed from record s5 + 1 in later columns; that record is the previous column's third vertex of pass j + 1, which is the same point. The second vertex is evaluated into the spare record when the column is the first or when j equals a2, and otherwise replayed from record s5, the previous column's third vertex of pass j. When the outer loop ends it runs one more strip along the last u line: u is +0x7a0 when the u count equals i or u1 plus i times du otherwise, where i is the end index a3 after a normal run and the lower index a1 when a1 was not below a3; Begin with 3, then for j from a4 down to a2 one vertex each at (that u, v at j), then End. Each is replayed by `sendChange` from table record s5 when s5 is between 0 and 1023 and evaluated into the spare record otherwise, s5 starting at the number of passes in the last column minus 1 and falling by 1 per vertex. After a normal run that count is a4 - a2 + 1 passes. When a1 was not below a3 no column ran, and s5 is the 32-bit stack word at 8(sp) minus 1, a word this routine never stores. When a4 is below a2 the final strip has no vertices, just Begin and End.

## Accumulation buffer

The context keeps one accumulation implementation, chosen when the buffer is set up: a 16-bit accumulation buffer is a host float RGBA array that the library adds into itself, and a 24-bit one is kept on the board in two scratch images and combined with the blender. The context's entry for the operation (+0x11e24) holds the routine, and `__glOdyim_Accum` calls it. The soft buffer record sits at +0x7ab8 (data pointer at +0x10, bytes per pixel at +0x18, row stride in pixels at +0x1c, allocator at +0x84); the accumulation rectangle is the four floats at +0x11e28 (x min), +0x11e2c (y min), +0x11e30 (x max) and +0x11e34 (y max). The accumulation operations use the public enumerants GL_ACCUM (0x100), GL_LOAD (0x101), GL_RETURN (0x102), GL_MULT (0x103) and GL_ADD (0x104). These routines move pixels through the pixel paths (`docs/pixel-paths.md`) and are read here only as far as their own arithmetic and state go: `softAccumulate`, `softLoad`, `softReturn`, `hardLoadAccumulate24`, `hardReturn24` and `hardClear24` are the pixel-path callers and are not read in this pass.

- `__glOdysseyInitAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd955de0, 224 bytes): **established.**
  Chooses the accumulation implementation for a new context (arguments: the buffer record, the context). It calls `__glInitBuffer` first. When the context's accumulation bit depth at +0xc9c is 16 it stores `__glOdySoftAccum` at +0x11e24, the clear routine `softClear` (0xd953730) at +0x6c of the record, 16 as the record's bytes per pixel and `__glSoftAllocAccum` as its allocator; when it is 24 it stores `__glOdyHardAccum24` and the clear routine at 0xd9557e0 and marks the two scratch objects absent (object numbers at +0x12064 and +0x120ac set to -1, their indexes and the words at +0x12068, +0x1206c, +0x120b0 and +0x120b4 to 0). Any other depth returns 0.
- `__glOdyim_Accum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd955ec0, 364 bytes): **established.**
  glAccum (arguments: the value as a float, the operation). It raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION (0x502) inside glBegin and glEnd, when the context has no accumulation buffer (the byte at +0xc98 is 0), in colour-index mode (+0xc95 nonzero) or when the byte at +0xdd0 is 0; an operation other than the five enumerants raises GL_INVALID_ENUM. Only in GL_RENDER mode (+0xc84 equal to 0x1c00) does it act: it saves the user's raster state and loads the library's own with `__glOdysseyImageSaveUserBuzzRasterState` and `__glOdysseyImageLoadBuzzRasterState`, sets the hardware buffers up with `initBuffersForAccum` once (the byte at +0x120f5), calls `initGeomForAccum`, calls the routine at +0x11e24 with the value and the operation, and restores the raster state.
- `__glSoftAllocAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9536f0, 64 bytes): **established.**
  Allocates the soft buffer's pixels: it calls the context's routine at +0x7af0 with the record and the window width and height from +0xe74 and +0xe78.
- `__glOdySoftAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd953a10, 240 bytes): **established.**
  The soft implementation's entry (arguments: the value, the operation). It first allocates the buffer through the record's allocator (+0x7b3c, with the record at +0x7ab8) when the data pointer at +0x7ac8 is 0, then jumps by operation: GL_ACCUM to `softAccumulate` (0xd951260), GL_LOAD to `softLoad` (0xd9507c0), GL_RETURN to `softReturn` (0xd952ae0), GL_MULT to `softMult` (0xd951d70) and GL_ADD to `softAdd` (0xd952460), each called with the value and the record.
- `softClear` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd953730, 728 bytes): **established.**
  Clears the accumulation rectangle of the soft buffer to the accumulation clear colour (argument: the buffer record). It allocates the data first through the record's routine at +0x84 when the pointer at +0x10 is 0, takes the four clear floats from +0x670 to +0x67c and writes them to every pixel of the rectangle (rows counted from the window top as height minus y max to height minus y min), with the row address formed from the record's pixel size and row stride; the inner loop is unrolled by four and by sixteen.
- `softMult` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd951d70, 1748 bytes): **established.**
  GL_MULT on the soft buffer (arguments: the multiplier as a float, the record). A multiplier of 1.0 does nothing; 0.0 stores zero in every component of the rectangle; any other multiplier multiplies every component, and when it lies outside -1 to 1 each product is also limited to -1.0 or 1.0. The loops are unrolled by 16 and by 4 components.
- `softAdd` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd952460, 1656 bytes): **established.**
  GL_ADD on the soft buffer (arguments: the addend as a float, the record). Zero does nothing; otherwise the addend is added to every component of the rectangle, and each sum is limited to -1.0 from below when the addend is negative and to 1.0 from above when it is positive.
- `__glOdyHardAccum24` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd955c10, 460 bytes): **established.**
  The 24-bit implementation's entry (arguments: the value, the operation). It builds the board buffers with `initBuffersForAccum` when the byte at +0x120f5 is clear, always calls `initAccumGfxImages` and `initScratchBufAccum` (returning when the latter fails), sets +0x16960 bit 21 and clears bit 20 of +0x96a4 and sets the global 0x1018 to 2. For every operation but GL_RETURN it saves the word +0x11cc0, replaces it with the word at +0x11d94 and puts it back afterwards. A nonzero value smaller than 1/4096 in magnitude is replaced by plus or minus 1/4096 (constants at 0xdaf4b38 and 0xdaf4b3c). GL_ACCUM and GL_LOAD call `hardLoadAccumulate24` (0xd953b00), GL_RETURN `hardReturn24` (0xd954a00), GL_MULT `hardMult24` (0xd954620) and GL_ADD `hardAdd24` (0xd9541a0).
- `sendBlenderOpTokens` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950000, 104 bytes): **established.**
  Sets the blender operation word (arguments: a 3-bit field, a 7-bit field, a 6-bit field). It replaces the low 16 bits of the context word +0x7c34 with the first argument in bits 15:13, the second in bits 12:6 and the third in bits 5:0, and marks +0x16960 bit 0, which `__glOdysseyValidateHardware` sends as block `0x10c21` (the three words from +0x7c34); the global 0x1018 becomes 2. Nothing is sent here. What each field means on the board (the factor selectors of the blend block) was not traced.
- `sendBlenderColorTokensAccum24` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950070, 116 bytes): **established.**
  Sets the blender's constant colour to one 12-bit value in all four channels (argument: the value). It stores the low 12 bits of the argument twice, in bits 11:0 and 23:12, in both +0x7c38 and +0x7c3c while keeping their top bytes, marks +0x16960 bit 0 and sets the global 0x1018 to 2; these are the second and third words of the same block `0x10c21`.
- `destroyScratchBufAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9500f0, 152 bytes): **established.**
  Releases the two scratch board objects of the 24-bit accumulation: `__glOdysseyDestroyPMObj` for the one at +0x12064 (its entry word +0x12068) and for the one at +0x120ac (entry +0x120b0); a failure of either raises GL_OUT_OF_MEMORY (0x505).
- `__glOdysseyAccumClose` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950190, 116 bytes): **established.**
  Forgets the accumulation images without destroying the objects (result 1). The two image object numbers at +0x11ec0 and +0x11efc go back to -1 (with their entry words +0x11ec4 and +0x11f00 cleared) when they were set; when the scratch object numbers at +0x12064 and +0x120ac are both -1 it only clears the byte +0x120f5; otherwise it also zeroes +0x120b0 and +0x12068 and sets both numbers to -1.
- `initScratchBufAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950210, 776 bytes): **established.**
  Makes sure the two scratch board objects of the 24-bit accumulation exist and match the window (result 1, or 0 with GL_OUT_OF_MEMORY after a failed create). When the window size (+0xe74, +0xe78) equals the size recorded at +0x12090 and +0x12094 and the byte +0x120f4 is clear, and both object numbers are set, it returns 1 at once. Otherwise it sets +0x120f4, destroys any existing objects with `destroyScratchBufAccum`, and creates two with `__glOdysseyCreatePMObj`, each described by a ten-word record with byte 0 equal to 6, byte 1 equal to 8, the width as int(x max + 1.0) and the height as int(y max + 1.0) of the accumulation rectangle and a depth word 1. It fills an image descriptor for each object, at +0x12070 and +0x120b8: flag bytes and words of 1, a copy of the object's number and entry words (+0x12080 to +0x12088 from +0x12064 to +0x1206c for the first), the window size (+0x12090 and +0x12094 for the first), two words of 2, the format code 0xc and a row length of twice ceil(width / 32) (constants 1/32 at 0xdaf4b04 and 2.0 at 0xdaf4d68); it records the window size and clears +0x120f4.
- `initVisualNumComp` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950520, 176 bytes): **established.**
  Stores a component count for the accumulation images in +0x12018, chosen from `__glOdysseyImageCalcFieldFormat` of the word at +0x11e08 (a seven-entry table at 0xdae8000): the value 3 gives 2; the values 0, 1, 2 and 4 give 1; 5 and 6 give 4 when the word at +0xd64 is nonzero and 3 otherwise; any value above 6 leaves +0x12018 alone. That the stored number is the count of colour components is **inferred**.
- `initAccumGfxImages` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9505d0, 240 bytes): **established.**
  Fills the two image descriptors the hardware accumulation draws from, at +0x11eb0 and +0x11eec. Both get flags of 1, the window width (+0xe74) and height (+0xe78), and a format code of 0xc. The first names the kernel's attach-record object -3 (word +0x11ec0, entry 4) with its address word from +0x16d74 and log2 of +0x16d70 minus 1 as the two size exponents; the second names object -4 (+0x11efc, entry 5) with +0x16d88 and log2 of +0x16d84 minus 1 (`__glOdysseyImageLogBase2`).
- `initBuffersForAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9506c0, 112 bytes): **established.**
  Prepares the drawable descriptions for the accumulation (no arguments). It calls `initVisualNumComp`, describes the window twice with `__glOdysseyImageDescribeWindow` (once for the descriptor at +0x11e38 with the second argument 1 and once for +0x11e74 with 0) and sets the byte +0x120f5.
- `initGeomForAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd950730, 144 bytes): **established.**
  Computes the accumulation rectangle in the hardware path's two work records (no arguments). It stores the rectangle twice, at +0x1201c and at +0x1202c, as four floats each in the order x min (from +0x11e28), y max plus 1.0 (+0x11e34 plus 1.0, truncated to an integer), x max plus 1.0 (from +0x11e30, truncated) and y min (from +0x11e2c); it stores the width and height (x max plus 1 minus x min, y max plus 1 minus y min) at +0x12048 and +0x1204c and again at +0x12058 and +0x1205c, and zeroes +0x12040, +0x12044, +0x12050, +0x12054, +0x1203c and +0x12060.

## Per-state setters and the board words they build

These routines keep the GL value in the generic part of the context and build the board's own encoding in the hardware-state words. They all return at once with GL_INVALID_OPERATION (0x502) inside glBegin and glEnd (global 0x1018 equal to 1), raise GL_INVALID_ENUM (0x500) or GL_INVALID_VALUE (0x501) for bad arguments, and store 2 in the global 0x1018 after a change. The words involved (offsets into the context record): +0x7bfc raster control, +0x7c04 polygon mode, +0x7c28 alpha test, +0x7c2c stencil, +0x7c30 depth test, +0x7c34, +0x7c38 and +0x7c3c the three words of block `0x021` (blender operation and constant colour), +0x7c40 logic operation, +0x7c44 to +0x7c4c line stipple and +0x7c5c the write masks. Where a routine sends a word, it sends the settle prefix `0x10443` 250 `0x10046` `0x10046` first when the settle-needed word +0x9830 is set (clearing it), then one class-1 block; `scripts/fifo_words.py` over each routine confirms the blocks named. Enumerant names are from the public OpenGL registry.

The following o32 entries were read independently from the complete listings; their addresses and sizes identify the o32 `libGLcore.so` rather than the N32 entries below.

- `__glOdyim_DrawBuffer` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95df88, 864 bytes): **established.** Validates `glDrawBuffer` against the current begin/end state, stereo and back-buffer bytes (+0xc97 and +0xc96), the left/right-back selector (+0x7b74), and the auxiliary count (+0xce0). It maps accepted front/back/left/right enumerants to four flags, calls the context entry at +0x760 with those flags, and stores the request at +0x764; auxiliary selections call the entry with all four flags clear. Invalid enumerants raise 0x500 and unavailable buffers raise 0x502. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95df88, including all 13 switch cases and their common call at 0xd95e2b4.
- `__glOdyim_Materialfv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95ec9c, 500 bytes): **established.** Validates the face and parameter through the material checker, raises its returned error, otherwise updates generic material state and sends the float values through the front/back `ApplyParameterF` entries. With colour material enabled (bit 7 of +0x700), it removes the tracked face/parameter pair, turns ambient-and-diffuse into its untracked component, and turns a tracked front-and-back update into the opposite face; shininess and colour-index parameters are not removed. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95ec9c, including each front, back and combined call.
- `__glOdyim_Materialf` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95ee90, 96 bytes): **established.** Accepts only GL_SHININESS (0x1601), stores the float in its stack argument slot and calls `__glOdyim_Materialfv`; another parameter raises GL_INVALID_ENUM (0x500). The complete o32 body is 24 instructions from 0xd95ee90 through 0xd95ef4c.
- `__glOdyim_Materialiv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95f4dc, 516 bytes): **established.** Converts the first integer at the supplied value pointer to float, validates and updates generic material state, then applies the same face/parameter suppression as the float form through `ApplyParameterI`, including separate front, back and combined calls. A failed material check raises its returned error; the complete o32 body was read at 0xd95f4dc.
- `__glOdyim_Materiali` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95f6e0, 96 bytes): **established.** Accepts only GL_SHININESS (0x1601), stores the integer in its stack argument slot and calls `__glOdyim_Materialiv`; another parameter raises GL_INVALID_ENUM (0x500). The complete o32 body is 24 instructions from 0xd95f6e0 through 0xd95f740.
- `__glOdysseyConvertStipple` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95fa34, 488 bytes): **established.** Converts the generic 32-row stipple into the context's 128-byte board copy in reverse row order, then sends the pending-settle prefix when +0x1831 is set, the mode word under 0x1043f, and the two 16-row blocks under 0x13c4e and 0x13c4f. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95fa34, including the source copy and all three FIFO writes.
- `__glOdyim_BlendColorEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd961618, 1132 bytes): **established.** Rejects calls inside begin/end with 0x502, clamps four colour floats to zero and the context colour scale at +0xdd8, stores them at +0x730 through +0x73c, converts each to a 12-bit field, and packs red/green into +0x7c38 and blue/alpha into +0x7c3c. It marks dirty bit 0 at +0x6960 and changes the global state word to 2; the complete o32 body was read through its four conversion and read-modify-write paths at 0xd961618.
- `__glOdyim_PolygonOffset` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd961a84, 112 bytes): **established.** Rejects calls inside begin/end with 0x502; otherwise multiplies the two float arguments and calls the context entry at +0x1fc with the product. It writes no FIFO word itself. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd961a84.
- `__glOdyim_PolygonOffsetEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd961af4, 724 bytes): **established.** Rejects calls inside begin/end with 0x502, stores the two factors at +0x1f4 and +0x1f8, recomputes the depth-scaled offset from the projection/depth-range records, clamps the resulting board word to the signed 24-bit range, and updates +0x7c50. It sends the settle prefix when +0x1831 is set, then 0x10458 and the new word; the complete o32 body was read at 0xd961af4.
- `__glOdyim_Bitmap` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd96367c, 300 bytes): **established.** Requires a validation-safe global state (0 or 2 when the dirty word at +0x16964 is clear); otherwise it raises 0x502. A pending validation marks +0x6975, calls the context validator at +0x2e40 and clears the global state. Negative width or height raises 0x501; valid dimensions call the bitmap entry at +0x3114 with the remaining arguments. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd96367c.
- `__glOdyim_PopAttrib` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d090, 188 bytes): **established.** Rejects begin/end with 0x502 and an empty hardware stack with 0x504. Otherwise it takes the hardware record below +0x68d4 and the generic record below +0x31bc, decrements both tops, and calls `PopAttributes`; the complete o32 body was read at 0xd95d090.
- `__glOdysseyFreeAttributeState` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d14c, 296 bytes): **established.** Pops the generic attribute stack through `__glOdyim_PopAttrib`, walks the hardware record-pointer array from +0x68d0 for the configured depth at +0xec0 and releases each non-null record through the context release entry at +0xc, then releases the array and clears +0x68d0. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95d14c.
- `__glOdyim_AlphaFunc` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d274, 492 bytes): **established.** Accepts comparison functions 0x200 through 0x207, clamps the reference to [0, +0xdd8], stores the function at +0x71c and the float at +0x720, packs the reference into the low 12 bits of +0x7c28 and sends block 0x1041e. Invalid functions raise 0x500 and begin/end raises 0x502. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95d274, including its conversion and settle branches.
- `__glOdyim_ColorMaterial` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d9ec, 304 bytes): **established.** Accepts front, back or front-and-back and the five material parameters ambient, diffuse, specular, emission and ambient-and-diffuse; invalid values raise 0x500 and begin/end raises 0x502. It stores the selections at +0x568 and +0x56c, calls the two material-colour callbacks when bit 7 of +0x700 is set, then calls the colour-material validator at context entry +0x2f18. Invalid face or mode raises 0x500; begin/end raises 0x502. The complete o32 body was read at 0xd95f87c, including the conditional settle prefix.

- `__glOdyim_BlendFunc` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95afe0, 780 bytes): **established.**
  glBlendFunc (arguments: source factor, destination factor). Each factor becomes a 6-bit code: GL_ZERO 0x00, GL_ONE 0x08, GL_DST_COLOR (source) and GL_SRC_COLOR (destination) 0x10, GL_SRC_ALPHA 0x18, GL_DST_ALPHA 0x20, GL_CONSTANT_COLOR 0x28, GL_CONSTANT_ALPHA 0x30, GL_ONE_MINUS_DST_COLOR (source) and GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_COLOR (destination) 0x38, GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA 0x39, GL_ONE_MINUS_DST_ALPHA 0x3a, GL_ONE_MINUS_CONSTANT_COLOR 0x3b and GL_ONE_MINUS_CONSTANT_ALPHA 0x3c; GL_SRC_ALPHA_SATURATE (source only) is 0x40. The same code table serves both factors, so the code names "the other buffer's colour" for the source and "the source colour" for the destination. The source code goes to bits 12:6 and the destination code to bits 5:0 of +0x7c34, the other bits kept; the GL values go to +0x724 and +0x728, bit 0 of +0x16960 is set (the validator sends block `0x10c21`) and nothing is sent here. Anything outside the table raises GL_INVALID_ENUM.
- `__glOdyim_BlendEquationEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95b2f0, 400 bytes): **established.**
  glBlendEquationEXT (argument: the equation). A 3-bit code goes to bits 15:13 of +0x7c34: GL_FUNC_ADD 0, GL_MIN 1, GL_MAX 2, GL_ALPHA_MIN_SGIX (0x8320) 3, GL_ALPHA_MAX_SGIX (0x8321) 4, GL_LOGIC_OP (0xbf1) 5, GL_FUNC_SUBTRACT 6 and GL_FUNC_REVERSE_SUBTRACT 7. The GL value goes to +0x72c and +0x16960 bit 0 is set; nothing is sent.
- `__glOdyim_BlendColorEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95f100, 472 bytes): **established.**
  glBlendColorEXT (arguments: four floats in f12, f13, f14 and f15, the first to fourth, called red, green, blue and alpha here in that order). When the begin-mode word at absolute address 4120 equals 1 (inside glBegin and glEnd) it calls `__glSetError` (local GOT entry 1490, 0xda34dc0) with 1282 and the current context from the word at 4112, and returns without storing anything. Otherwise each float is limited below at 0.0 and above at the float at context +0xdd8 (3544), by compares that pass a NaN through, and the four results are stored in the order fourth, third, second, first at context +0x73c, +0x738, +0x734 and +0x730. Each limited value is then turned into a 12-bit field: when the value converted to double is at least 1.0 (the double at 0xdaf4d78) the field is 4095 (the float 4095.0 at 0xdaf4b14), otherwise the value times 4096.0 (the float at 0xdaf4b18) truncated to a 64-bit integer. The fields go into two words by read-modify-write, four stores in this order: the word at +0x7c38 (31800) takes the first value in bits 23:12 (that store keeps bits 31:24 and 11:0) and then the second value in bits 11:0 (the second store keeps bits 31:12 of the word the first store wrote); the word at +0x7c3c (31804) takes the third value in bits 23:12 and then the fourth in bits 11:0 the same way. So red and green share +0x7c38 and blue and alpha share +0x7c3c, and bits 31:24 of both words are untouched. It then sets bit 0 of the word at +0x16960 and stores 2 into the begin-mode word at 4120 (in the delay slot of the return), the validate-pending state. It saves and restores gp in a 16-byte frame and sets no return value.

The function must be one of the eight GL_NEVER to GL_ALWAYS values (0x200 to 0x207) and goes, minus 0x200, to bits 14:12 of +0x7c28; the reference is limited between 0 and the colour scale at +0xdd8, stored as a float at +0x720, and converted to 12 bits (value times 4096 when below 1.0, else 4095) into bits 11:0; bits 31:15 are kept and the GL function goes to +0x71c. The depth-pass code goes to bits 18:16, the depth-fail code to bits 21:19 and the stencil-fail code to bits 24:22 of +0x7c2c, the other bits kept; the GL values go to +0x68c, +0x690 and +0x694 and +0x16960 bit 5 is set.
