A 13-entry jump table at 0xdae8020 (found with `ghidra/scripts/IrixJumpTables.java`) decodes 0x400 to 0x40c: GL_FRONT_LEFT, GL_FRONT and GL_LEFT store GL_FRONT at +0x4d4; GL_BACK_LEFT and GL_BACK need the back-buffer byte +0xc96 and store GL_BACK; GL_FRONT_RIGHT and GL_RIGHT need the stereo byte +0xc97 and store the value as given; GL_BACK_RIGHT needs both; GL_AUX0 to GL_AUX3 need the auxiliary-buffer count at +0xce0 to exceed the index and store the value; GL_FRONT_AND_BACK and everything outside the range raise GL_INVALID_ENUM. A missing buffer raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION (and for the stereo test of GL_FRONT_RIGHT the value is still stored after the error). The requested value goes to +0x4d8, the global is set to 2 and +0x2e24 gets bit 4.

## 64-bit state setters re-read from the shipped binary

- `__glOdyim_IsEnabled` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ea9f0, 680 bytes): **established.**
  Answers the enabled-state query for board-owned enumerants from the current context, returning 0 or 1. It tests capability bits at +0x704 and +0x718, the preclip byte at +0x19db4 and the eight fragment-light bits at +0x1647c; unsupported enumerants tail-call the generic query, while a query inside begin/end raises 0x502.
- `__glOdyim_Hint` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31eaca0, 212 bytes): **established.**
  Accepts hint modes 0x1100 through 0x1102, rejects other modes with GL_INVALID_ENUM, and handles target 0x83ef by storing the mode at +0x19db8. When that value changes with +0x19db4 enabled it marks +0x19dc0, dirty bit 10 at +0x16964 and the validation state; other targets call the generic hint routine.
- `__glOdyim_AlphaFunc` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31eefd0, 324 bytes): **established.**
  Validates the comparison function in 0x200..0x207, packs it into bits 14:12 of +0x7c28, clamps the reference to the colour scale at +0xdd8 and converts it to the 12-bit field at +0x7c28 bits 11:0. It stores the function at +0x71c and sends block 0x1041e with +0x7c28; invalid values call `__glSetError` with 0x500.
- `__glOdyim_BlendFunc` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef120, 788 bytes): **established.**
  Maps each source and destination blend factor to its six-bit hardware code, stores the packed pair in +0x7c34 and the original enumerants at +0x724 and +0x728, and marks dirty bit 0 at +0x16960 for block 0x10c21. Unsupported factors call `__glSetError` with 0x500 and send nothing.
- `__glOdyim_BlendEquationEXT` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef440, 404 bytes): **established.**
  Converts the eight accepted blend equations 0x8006, 0x8007, 0x8008, 0x8320, 0x8321, 0xbf1, 0x800a and 0x800b to bits 15:13 of +0x7c34, stores the original value at +0x72c and marks dirty bit 0 at +0x16960. An unlisted value raises GL_INVALID_ENUM.
- `__glOdyim_ColorMask` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef5e0, 256 bytes): **established.**
  Stores the four enable flags as bytes at +0x75c through +0x75f, mirrors them into bits 20 through 23 of +0x7c5c and marks dirty bit 7 at +0x16960. The four argument paths use a read-modify-write of +0x7c5c.
- `__glOdyim_ColorMaterial` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef6e0, 276 bytes): **established.**
  Accepts the three face values and five material parameters, stores them at +0x568 and +0x56c, and rejects other values with GL_INVALID_ENUM. If colour material is enabled at bit 7 of +0x700 it calls the context entries at +0x2e48 and +0x2f18, then calls `ValidateColorMaterial`.
- `__glOdyim_CullFace` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef800, 260 bytes): **established.**
  Encodes GL_BACK, GL_FRONT and GL_FRONT_AND_BACK in bits 10:9 of +0x7bfc, stores the original face at +0x1ec, sets dirty bit 1 at +0x16960 and sets bit 2 at +0x2e24. Other values call `__glSetError` with 0x500.
- `__glOdyim_DepthFunc` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31ef910, 248 bytes): **established.**
  Accepts comparison values 0x200 through 0x207, stores the value at +0x660, copies its low three bits into +0x7c30 and sends block 0x10420. It raises GL_INVALID_ENUM for other values and sets bit 11 at +0x16964 after a valid update.
- `__glOdyim_DepthMask` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31efa10, 180 bytes): **established.**
  Stores the flag byte at +0x664 and sets bit 24 of +0x7c5c only when the flag is nonzero and the visual's depth-buffer byte at +0xc99 is set. It marks dirty bit 7 at +0x16960 and returns without a FIFO write.
- `__glOdyim_ReadBuffer` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31efad0, 416 bytes): **established.**
  Decodes the read-buffer range 0x400..0x40c, checks back-buffer, stereo and auxiliary-buffer availability at +0xc96, +0xc97 and +0xce0, stores the request at +0x4d8 and the selected board value at +0x4d4, then sets validation state 2 and bit 4 at +0x2e24. Invalid values raise GL_INVALID_ENUM; unavailable buffers raise GL_INVALID_OPERATION.
- `__glOdyim_FrontFace` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31f01f0, 224 bytes): **established.**
  Accepts GL_CW and GL_CCW, sets or clears bit 23 of +0x7bfc, stores the original winding at +0x1f0, sets dirty bit 1 at +0x16960 and sets bit 2 at +0x2e24. Other values raise GL_INVALID_ENUM.
- `__glOdyim_LineStipple` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31f0370, 268 bytes): **established.**
  Stores the 16-bit pattern at +0x1e0 and the repeat factor clamped to 1..256 at +0x1e2, copies the pattern into the low 16 bits of +0x7c44 and +0x7c48, puts factor minus one in +0x7c4c and sends block 0x10c48.
- `__glOdyim_LineWidth` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31f0480, 240 bytes): **established.**
  Rejects nonpositive widths with GL_INVALID_VALUE, stores the float at +0x1d4, rounds an integer copy at +0x1dc, clamps another copy at +0x1d8 using the limits at +0xeac and +0xeb0, and marks bit 11 at +0x16964. No FIFO word is stored on this path.
- `__glOdyim_LogicOp` (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x31f0570, 220 bytes): **established.**
  Accepts the sixteen values 0x1500..0x150f, stores the original operation at +0x740, copies its low four bits to +0x7c40 bits 3:0 and sends block 0x10422. Values outside the range raise GL_INVALID_ENUM.

## Draw-buffer, material and polygon-stipple setters

The routines below were read from the decompilation next to the assembly and then run in an emulator one input at a time (`scripts/glcore_sweep.py` for the decision trees, `scripts/fifo_words.py` for the command words). The emulator keeps 32-bit floating-point registers, so the shininess and colour-index branches of the two material-parameter routines, which hold doubles in odd-numbered registers, were read from the assembly and their command words checked by hand.

- `__glOdyim_DrawBuffer` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95bad0, 1356 bytes): **established.**
  glDrawBuffer (argument: the buffer enumerant). Inside glBegin and glEnd (global 0x1018 equal to 1) it raises GL_INVALID_OPERATION (0x502). The enumerants are 0, GL_FRONT_LEFT to GL_BACK_RIGHT (0x400 to 0x403), GL_FRONT, GL_BACK, GL_LEFT, GL_RIGHT, GL_FRONT_AND_BACK (0x404 to 0x408) and GL_AUX0 to GL_AUX3 (0x409 to 0x40c); anything else raises GL_INVALID_ENUM (0x500). Which ones are allowed depends on three bytes and a count of the visual: the stereo byte +0xc97, the back-buffer byte +0xc96, the byte +0x7b74 that decides whether the left and right selectors also take the back buffers, and the auxiliary-buffer count +0xce0. A forbidden one raises 0x502. On success it stores the effective buffer in +0x760, calls `__glOdysseySetDrawbuf` with four flags (front left, front right, back left, back right) and then stores the requested enumerant in +0x764. With the stereo byte set every enumerant keeps its own name in +0x760 and the flags follow the name: 0x400 to 0x403 one flag each, 0x404 front left and right, 0x405 back left and right, 0x406 and 0x407 front left or right (plus back left or right when +0x7b74 is set), 0x408 all four when +0x7b74 is set and front left and right otherwise. Without stereo the right-hand buffers 0x401, 0x403 and 0x407 are errors; with only the back-buffer byte 0x400, 0x404 and 0x406 store GL_FRONT in +0x760 and set the front-left flag, 0x402 and 0x405 store GL_BACK and set back left, and 0x406 and 0x408 store 0x408 and set front left plus back left when +0x7b74 is set; with neither byte every back enumerant is an error and 0x400, 0x404, 0x406 and 0x408 store GL_FRONT and set front left. GL_AUX0 to GL_AUX3 are accepted when their index is below +0xce0, store themselves and send four zero flags; the others raise 0x502. Enumerant 0 clears all four flags and stores 0.
- `__glOdyim_Materialfv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95c900, 488 bytes): **established.**
  glMaterialfv (arguments: face, parameter, pointer to four floats). It validates the pair with `__glErrorCheckMaterial` and raises the error number it returns, stores the values in the generic material state with `__glim_Materialfv`, and then sends them to the board through `ApplyParameterF`, once with side 1 for the front face (0x404) and once with side 0 for the back (0x405), both for GL_FRONT_AND_BACK (0x408). When colour material is on (bit 7 of the word at +0x700) the board must not be given the pair that the current colour already drives (face at +0x568, parameter at +0x56c), so the call is cut down first: the tracked parameter of the tracked face is dropped; GL_AMBIENT_AND_DIFFUSE (0x1602) becomes the one member that is not tracked; and GL_FRONT_AND_BACK becomes the face that is not tracked. When ambient-and-diffuse is tracked for a face, ambient, diffuse and ambient-and-diffuse calls for that face send nothing. Shininess (0x1601) and the colour indexes (0x1603) are never cut. The emulated sweep over three faces, seven parameters and sixteen colour-material settings produced exactly these calls.
- `__glOdyim_Materialf` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95caf0, 84 bytes): **established.**
  glMaterialf (arguments: face, parameter, a float). Only GL_SHININESS (0x1601) is accepted; any other parameter raises GL_INVALID_ENUM (0x500). The float is kept in a stack slot and `__glOdyim_Materialfv` is called with the face, the parameter and the slot's address.
- `__glOdyim_Materialiv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d0b0, 496 bytes): **established.**
  glMaterialiv, with the control flow of `__glOdyim_Materialfv`: `__glErrorCheckMaterial` receives the first integer as its fourth argument, the generic state is set through `__glim_Materialiv`, and the board calls go to `ApplyParameterI` with the same side, face and colour-material cut-down. The sweep that produced the table for the float routine, run on this routine, gave an identical list of calls for all 3 x 7 x 16 combinations.
- `__glOdyim_Materiali` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d2a0, 84 bytes): **established.**
  glMateriali (arguments: face, parameter, an integer). It accepts only GL_SHININESS (0x1601), raising 0x500 for anything else, keeps the integer in a stack slot and calls `__glOdyim_Materialiv` with that slot.
- `ApplyParameterF` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95c470, 1168 bytes): **established.**
  Sends one material parameter to the board (arguments: side, 1 for front and 0 for back; the parameter enumerant; a pointer to four floats). For ambient (0x1200), diffuse (0x1201), specular (0x1202), emission (0x1600) and ambient-and-diffuse (0x1602, both of the first two) it sends a four-word block per parameter, the block word being `0xc080d000` OR the register OR a count of 6 to 13 shifted left 24, followed by the four floats unchanged: front 0xc680d02f, 0xc780d030, 0xc880d031 and 0xc980d032, back 0xca80d035, 0xcb80d036, 0xcc80d037 and 0xcd80d038, for ambient, diffuse, specular and emission. In a colour-index visual (the byte +0xc94 is 0) these four are not sent. Shininess (0x1601) is raised to 2.5 when it lies between 0 and 2.5, its square root is stored as a float at +0x9814 (front) or +0x9818 (back), and when that changed the routine sends `0xd080c433` (front) or `0xd080c439` (back) with the square root, then `0x908091ea` with the two cached roots each followed by its negation, and calls `__glOdysseySetProgram` with the current program (+0xa6a8). The colour indexes (0x1603) matter only in a colour-index visual: with the three indexes a, d and s (the third is zero when absent) it sends the four register blocks with the values a, d - a + 0.5 and s - a + 0.5 and 0 as the first word of each and zeros after it.
- `ApplyParameterI` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95cb50, 1368 bytes): **established.**
  The integer form of `ApplyParameterF` with the same side, parameter and register blocks, with three differences. Every call first sends the settle prefix when +0x9830 asks and then block `0x13c4d` with the sixteen words at +0x7c70 to +0x7cac, even when no parameter follows. Colour values are converted: each integer, as a float, becomes 1.0 (the literal at 0xdaf4b00) plus 2.0 (0xdaf4b40) times the integer and is then multiplied by the scale word at +0xdf0, which is the (2c + 1) / (2^32 - 1) rule when that word holds 1 / (2^32 - 1). The shininess branch always sends its words and caches the square root without comparing it with the old one.
- `__glOdysseyConvertStipple` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd95d580, 456 bytes): **established.**
  Sends the polygon stipple to the board (argument: the context). It calls the generic `__glConvertStipple` to fill the 32 rows at +0x7570, then copies them to the 128-byte stipple block at +0x7b78 in reverse row order (row 0 goes to +0x7bf4, row 31 to +0x7b78, a run of the routine with markers in the first, second and last source words showed it). It sends the settle prefix when +0x9830 is set, `0x1043f` with the stipple mode word at +0x7bf8, and then the two pattern blocks `0x13c4e` with the first sixteen rows (+0x7b78 to +0x7bb4) and `0x13c4f` with the last sixteen (+0x7bb8 to +0x7bf4).

## Fast-dispatch code buffers and their installation

The library's generated entry points for the current-value calls (colour, normal, texture coordinate, vertex) sit in one code region that `InitFastDispatch` makes writable. The routines below fill that region with one of several prepared sets of code, depending on the projection class, so a call such as `glColor3f` runs the code written for the current visual. The process record at the fixed address 0x17b4 holds the bookkeeping: +4 is the start of the region (the address of `glColor3b`), +0 its length, +8 the installed set (-1 for none), +0xc, +0x18, +0x20 and +0x28 the sizes 0x1400, 0x500, 0x1000 and 0x1800 of the colour, normal, texture-coordinate and vertex sets, and +0x10, +0x14, +0x1c, +0x24, +0x2c, +0x30 and +0x34 the pointers to the prepared copies (colour, colour index, normal, texture coordinate, vertex set 0, vertex set 2, vertex set 1).

- `CopyColorIndexCode` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd966080, 1032 bytes): **established.**
  Lays 40 code templates end to end in one buffer (arguments: the destination, the table of template addresses passed by value in the argument registers and on the stack, and the size of one template on the stack). It calls `memcpy` 39 times and ends with a tail call of the 40th, each copy the template size (0x80 bytes in the caller `AllocInitFastDispatchCodeBuffers`, which gives a 0x1400-byte set) and each starting where the last ended. Which table it is handed decides whether the result serves the RGBA colour calls or the colour-index calls.
- `CopyNormalCode` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd966490, 312 bytes): **established.**
  The same copy for the normal calls: ten templates (nine `memcpy` calls and a tail call of the tenth) from the table in `__glOdyim_normalDispatchTable`, each the size in its last argument (0x80 bytes in the caller), 0x500 bytes in all.
- `CopyTexCoordCode` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9665d0, 840 bytes): **established.**
  The same copy for the texture-coordinate calls: 32 templates (31 `memcpy` calls and a tail call of the last) from `__glOdyim_texCoordDispatchTable`, 0x80 bytes each in the caller, which fills the 0x1000-byte set.
- `CopyVertexCode` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd966920, 680 bytes): **established.**
  Copies the vertex templates in two parts chosen by a flag word on the stack (arguments: the destination, the table by value, the template size 0x100 and the flags). Bit 0 copies the first sixteen templates, bit 1 copies the last eight after them, so a flag of 3 fills a 0x1800-byte set and a flag of 1 or 2 only its first 0x1000 or last 0x800 bytes. It makes 24 `memcpy` calls in all and returns after the last.
- `AllocInitFastDispatchCodeBuffers` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd966bd0, 2124 bytes): **established.**
  Builds, on first use, the prepared code sets for one fast-dispatch mode (arguments: a record whose first word is the host's allocator callback, and a pointer to the mode number, which it rewrites). A negative mode does nothing. Otherwise any of the colour (+0x10), colour-index (+0x14), normal (+0x1c) and texture-coordinate (+0x24) sets that the process record at 0x17b4 does not hold yet is allocated through the callback with the size in the record and filled with `CopyColorIndexCode` (from `__glOdyim_colorDispatchTable` and `__glOdyim_indexDispatchTable`), `CopyNormalCode` and `CopyTexCoordCode`, and the vertex set for the mode is built into the record's slot: mode 0 at +0x2c from `__glOdyim_vertexDispatchTable` with flag 3, mode 2 at +0x30 from `__glOdyim_vertexSmallWDispatchTable` with flag 3, and mode 1 at +0x34 as the first part of the first table (flag 1) followed 0x1000 bytes on by the second part of the small-w table (flag 2). A failed allocation stores -1 in the mode and returns.
- `FastDispatchInstall` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd967620, 724 bytes): **established.**
  Switches the code region to the set the context now needs (argument: the context). It does nothing unless the byte at +0x19de0 is set. The wanted mode is -1 when the word at +0x137c is nonzero, or when the word at +0x16988 has a bit other than bit 4, the process word at 0x1048 is nonzero, the current-context word at 0x1010 is zero or the projection class at +0x19dbc is 3; otherwise it is the class. When the wanted mode is the installed one (record +8) it returns. A non-negative mode first calls `AllocInitFastDispatchCodeBuffers`, whose result may fall back to -1. With -1 and a set installed it copies the saved original code back over the region (from the copy `InitFastDispatch` keeps at gp-13604) and clears the word at 0x17b8. With a mode, when nothing is installed yet (record +8 is -1) it first copies the colour set (+0x10 when the RGBA byte +0xc94 is set, +0x14 otherwise), the normal set and the texture-coordinate set one after another from the region's start; for any mode it then copies the mode's vertex set (0x1800 bytes, the slot of mode 0, 1 or 2) to the address of `glVertex2d` and sets 0x17b8 to 1, and on that first install it adds 0x80 bytes each from the entries at +0xa0 and +0xa4 of the colour or index table behind the vertex code. It ends with `cacheflush` over the region (protection argument 3) and stores the new mode at record +8.

## Shadow-value conversion

- `ConvertFromShadow` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd96fce0, 2008 bytes): **established.**
  Turns one value held in the hardware shadow into four floats (arguments: the context, the output of four floats, the shadow's kind number from 6 to 58, and the source words). A kind below 6 or above 58 changes nothing. The 53 kinds go through a jump table at 0xdae8b30 (the same kind numbers `__glOdyDecodeShadowFormat` decodes, listed in `docs/opengl.md`), and the 29 distinct bodies are, by kind: 6 and 7 one integer or float into the first output only; 8 three words as indexes (times four) into the float array at +0xef0 of the context, then 1.0, and 15 four such lookups; 14, 35 and 43 three floats then 1.0 (and 21 and 37 four floats) copied as they are, 25, 52 and 58 three floats with the fourth output left alone; 22, 23 and 24 three signed bytes (read from the last byte of each word), halves (low half) or words converted to floats with the fourth left alone; 30 to 36 even kinds an integer and 31 to 37 odd kinds a float of one to four components with the missing ones 0, 0, 1.0 (30 and 31 one component, 32 and 33 two, 34 and 35 three with 1.0 last, 36 and 37 four); 11, 12 and 13 three signed words (2x + 1) times the scales at +0xde0, +0xde8 and +0xdf0 with 1.0 last, 18, 19 and 20 the same for four words (kind 20 forms 1.0 + 2.0 x instead), and 9 and 10 (three) and 16 and 17 (four) unsigned words times the scale at +0xde8 (9, 16) or +0xdf0 (10, 17). Every other kind, 26 to 29, 38 to 51, 53 to 57, returns without writing. The scale words hold the integer-to-colour factors the context sets up. Most bodies were run with markers and chosen scale words in an emulator (`scripts/glcore_sweep.py`); the unsigned conversions (9, 10, 16 and 17) use 64-bit moves the emulator does not run and were read from the assembly.
- `BeforeIgloo` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9da480, 2776 bytes): **established.**
  Rebuilds the software copies of the current material values from the hardware shadow before the vertex-array code runs (no arguments; the context is the process word at 0x1010). It does nothing but set the byte at +0x19e88 when the visual is colour-index (byte +0xc94 zero). Otherwise it asks `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadow` for the shadow, copies its 2304 bytes from the pointer at +0x16d28 to the stack, and uses eight two-bit selectors of two shadow words (word +0x84 bits 17:16, 15:14, 13:12, 11:10, 5:4, 3:2 and 1:0, word +0x88 bits 31:30; four saved values of 16 bytes sit behind each selector) to choose one 16-byte value per slot and copy it to the context at +0x19de8, +0x19df8, +0x19e08, +0x19e18, +0x19e38, +0x19e48, +0x19e58 and +0x19e68. It then calls the dispatch table's material entry (+0x80) for the front (0x404) and back (0x405) faces with the unit vector 0, 0, 0, 1.0 for ambient, diffuse and specular (0x1200 to 0x1202), decodes the current colour from a third shadow selector (word +0x84 bits 29:28 choose a descriptor, its kind in the low ten bits, 8 to 21, picks one of 14 bodies of a jump table at 0xdae95dc that turn the descriptor into four floats, several of them integer-to-colour conversions with the scales at +0xde0, +0xde8 and +0xdf0) and sends it to both faces as emission (0x1600, table value 5632 in the code). When colour material is on (bit 7 of +0x700) it writes the colour into the saved slot that the face (+0x568) and parameter (+0x56c) of colour material name (ambient, diffuse, emission or ambient-and-diffuse). It ends by setting the byte at +0x19e88.
- `MakeCurrentRead` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd96af00, 4500 bytes): **inferred** for the purpose of each step, **established** for the calls, tables and command words it issues.
  Binds the context to a draw and a read drawable (arguments: the context, the draw drawable, the read drawable, ...). It sets +0xdd0 to 1 when the two are the same, copies the 0x28-byte geometry records of the draw and read drawables (the pointer at drawable +4) into the context at +0xc90 and +0xd30 unless the cached flag at +0x16978 is set and the first word is unchanged, sets that flag, stores the context in the process words 0x1010 and 0x1018, and calls the drawable-setup entry at +0x38. For a drawable that entry turns into a pixmap it returns early when bit 1 of +0xa8 is set or the draw and read drawables differ, otherwise it installs the generic display-list tables (compiler, optimiser, `__glNop`, `__gl_GenericDlOps` and `__glListExecTable` at +0x134c to +0x1368) and finishes with `__glPixmapMakeCurrent`. For a window it points the dispatch table at +0x17f4 to the context's own copy at +0x1f60, validates the context with `__glOdysseyValidateContext`, calls the entries at +0x28 and +0x34, copies the full or the colour-index dispatch tables with `__glMipsCopyDispatch` (and the preclip bounding-box table when +0x19db4 is set), calls `__glMakeCurrentBuffers`, `ApplyViewport`, `ApplyScissor`, `__glOdyim_DrawBuffer` with the saved +0x764, and `__glContextSetColorScales`, copies a saved attribute record with `__glOdyssey_ResolveContext` when +0x168c8 is set, installs the fast-dispatch set through `FastDispatchInstall` (after `InitFastDispatch` on the first context), calls `__glOdysseyDriverMakeCurrent`, describes the draw and read windows with `__glOdysseyImageDescribeWindow` at +0x11d98 and +0x11dd4, merges their format fields into the four words at +0x969c to +0x96a8 with `__glOdysseyImageCalcFieldFormat`, sets +0x7b74 from the stereo field (1, or bit 21 of +0x7b70 for the three-bit field value 3), and sends two settle sequences around the frame descriptor block `0x11017` with those four words (preceded, when bit 27 of the third word is clear, by the single word `0x90008116`, otherwise by blocks `0x10841`, `0x1204b` with 0xffffff and `0x10840`). It sets bits 0 and 1 of +0xa8, clears bit 2 and returns 1; a failed step returns 0 and a failed validation calls the entry at +0x30 first. The first-time set-up (`HWInit`, `SWInit`, `InitialFree`, `InitFastDispatch`) is reached through the same routine for a context whose bit 0 of +0xa8 is clear.

## Context set-up, tear-down and drawable binding

- `GetBoardInfo` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd965310, 1448 bytes): **established.**
  Reads the board description from the kernel (`__glOdysseyGetBoardInfoRaw`) and turns it into the context's capability words and tunables (argument: the context). It writes the renderer name into +0x7b50 with the format `VPRO/%c/%d%s`, where the letter is chosen by the board kind at +0x16a70 (0 gives A, 1 gives B, 2 gives N, anything else ?), the number is +0x16a54, and the suffix is one of two short strings of the library by +0x16a68. The capability word +0x7b70 starts at 0x10; kind 0 makes it 0x410 and sets the word at +0x19dd4 to 10000 and +0x21eec to 0x40; a second board count (+0x16a60 at least 2) or any kind other than 0 adds bits 0 and 1, and a kind other than 0 also adds 0x8003a0, +0x19dd4 of 0x27100 and +0x21eec of 0x80; a kind of 2 or more adds the bits 0x7f0000. Always +0x19ddc is 5, +0x19dd8 is 40000 and +0xe8c, the largest texture dimension, is 0x800 for +0x16a54 of at least 0x80 and 0x200 otherwise. Environment variables then override: `GL_ODY_FAST_CLEAR` (bit 4 of +0x7b70), `GL_ODY_FAST_CLEAR_SUBMASK` (bit 6), `GL_ODY_COPY_PIXELS` (bit 1), `GL_ODY_FRONTBACK_OCTBYTES` (bit 21) and `GL_ODY_SCISSORING_WORKS` (bit 20) and `GL_ODY_HORIZ_VERT_LINES_NEAR0` (bit 22), each set by a value starting with y or Y and cleared by any other non-empty value; `GL_ODY_FAST_CLEAR_THRESHOLD` and `GL_ODY_DMA_DRAW_PIXELS_THRESHOLD` (integers, into +0x19dd4 and +0x19dd8); `GL_ODY_ENABLE_DIRECT_DMA` and `GL_ODY_NO_UNNECESSARY_DITHER` (bytes +0x19dcf and +0x19dd0, set unless the value starts with n); `GL_ODY_AUTO_MIPMAP_LEVELS_CUTOFF` (an integer of at least 0 into +0x19ddc); `GL_ODY_FAST_GLVIEWPORT` (byte +0x19de1, set by y or Y); `GL_ODY_FAST_VALIDATE` (byte +0x19de3, cleared by n or N); `GL_ODY_DISABLE_FAST_DLIST` (byte +0x19de4, cleared by y or Y); `GL_ODY_REVB_SMALL_TILES_COPYPIXEL` (byte +0x19de5, set by y or Y).
- `SWInit` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd96a950, 1452 bytes): **established.**
  Initialises the software side of a new context (argument: the context). It calls `InitBuffers`, `InitFunctionPtrs`, `__glSoftResetContext`, then sets +0x1fc to 2^-23, +0xe5c to 1/256 and +0xe60 to +0xdd4 minus 1/256, validates the context, calls `__glOdysseyInitHyperpipeState` at +0x21ef0, and reads the environment: `GL_ODY_STANDARD_SELECTION` (+0x168d8 is 0 when present, 1 otherwise), `GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_LIMIT_ANGLE` (a float in [0, 90), default 70, whose tangent is stored at +0x21ef4), `GL_VERTEX_PRECLIP` (DISABLED, FASTEST or NICEST, held as 0x1100, 0x1101 or 0x1102 at +0x19db8 with the enable byte +0x19db4; the projection class +0x19dbc becomes 0 when disabled, 2 for FASTEST and 3 for NICEST), `GL_ODY_VERTEX_PRECLIP_W_LIMIT` (+0x19d90, default 0.0625), `GL_ODY_VERTEX_PRECLIP_MAG_LIMIT` (+0x19d94, default 8192), `GL_ODY_VERTEX_PRECLIP_LINE0_EPSILON` (+0x19da4, default 1/512), `GL_ODY_VERTEX_PRECLIP_LINE0_LIMIT` (+0x19da8, default 1/128), `GL_ODY_DLIST_BBOX_VERTICES` (+0x19dc4), `GL_DISABLE_FAST_DISPATCH` (+0x19de0, set only when the variable is absent and the address of `glColor3b` is 16 KB aligned; an unaligned one prints a warning through the callback at +0x10) and `GL_ENABLE_PROJECTION_WAR` (+0x19dcc). It stores the extension string at +0xe00, the renderer at +0xdf8 (the `VPRO/` name from `GetBoardInfo`), the version string `1.2 Irix 6.5` at +0xdfc, sets +0x19dce to 0 and +0x1695c to 0 and calls the context entry +0x2e40; a failing step returns 0. The extension string names GL_ARB_imaging, EXT blend, convolution, copy_texture, histogram, packed_pixels, polygon_offset, subtexture, texture, texture3D, texture_env_add, texture_object and vertex_array, and SGI, SGIS and SGIX entries for color matrix and table, detail texture, fog function and offset, fragment lighting, pixel texture, texture lod and its bias, scale-bias, list priority, async, subsample, resample, interlace and vertex preclip.
- `GL_ENABLE_PROJECTION_WAR` and context byte +0x19dcc (N32 build `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`; the string is present once in the o32 and 64-bit builds too, whose code was not read): **established** for the store and the readers, **inferred** for the purpose.
  The context set-up stores 0 at +0x19dcc (0xd96ad60) and, when the call it makes with the string "GL_ENABLE_PROJECTION_WAR" returns nonzero, stores 1 (0xd96ad78); the value of the variable is not examined, so any setting turns it on and it is off by default. The byte has two readers in this build. (1) `__glOdysseyUpdateTransform` (0xd9e2980) loads it at 0xd9e29a0; when it is nonzero and the word at +64 of the projection matrix record (the record at context +29724) is 4, the value the public sample implementation gives its identity matrix type (`ogl-sample/gfx/lib/opengl/include/xform.h`), the routine takes a separate path at 0xd9e2c8c: it reads the record at context +29716 (the same record `__glOdysseyValidateMatrixComb` reads at +176 for the combined matrix), tests the floats at +12, +28 and +44 of it against zero, copies the 22 words at +176 on, calls `__glInvertTransposeMatrix` (0xda57680, GOT local entry 1753) on that matrix, and ends by storing 1 in the byte at +0x19dcd (0xd9e2e3c). Every other path of the routine stores 0 there (0xd9e29d4, 0xd9e2ed4). (2) The static routine the symbol table calls `dirtyMatrix` (0xd9e3b90, 172 bytes; the routine listed before it, `__glOdysseyValidateMatrixTex`, ends at 0xd9e3b8c, so a read at 0xd9e3bc8 is not that routine's) loads the byte at 0xd9e3bc8 in its case for matrix mode 0x1700 (GL_MODELVIEW) and, when it is nonzero, stores 1 in the byte at +0x16740 (0xd9e3bdc).
  The byte +0x19dcd is read by `__glOdysseyValidateSoftClipPlanes` (0xd9ee360; load at 0xd9ee5b8) and by `__glOdysseyVertexWARClip` (0xd9ef330; load at 0xd9ef3e8). In both, a nonzero value selects stores of two float constants into the record at context +1748 at +0x88 and +0x8c and sets bit 8 of a context word (0x100 ORed in at 0xd9ee5d4 and 0xd9ef410), where zero skips them. What those constants and that bit do on the board was not traced.
  So the workaround concerns a projection that is the identity while the whole transform sits in the modelview matrix: with it on, the library derives its clip-plane data from the modelview matrix in that case, which is **inferred** from the identity test and the matrix the path reads, not read from a comment. An orthographic projection applied through the modelview matrix, with the projection left as the identity, is the application pattern this fits, and a published report of lost depth under an early release (`docs/published-sources.md`) is consistent with it; whether the 6.5.9 library lacked the path or had it on by default is unknown, because no 6.5.9 library is in the install and the shipped 6.5.30 media, patch 4103 and patch 4104 carry the same string.

## Get routines

- `__glOdyim_GetTexLevelParameterfv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd985380, 848 bytes): **established.**

Inside glBegin and glEnd it raises 0x502; a target with no current texture object raises 0x500; a level outside 0 to the context's level count (+0xe94) or a parameter it does not know raises 0x501. The level record is the 0x44-byte entry at +0x554 + 0x44 x level of the texture object. The parameters read it as: GL_TEXTURE_WIDTH (0x1000) the word at +0x1c; GL_TEXTURE_HEIGHT (0x1001) +0x20, minus twice the border (+0x28) when `GetTexDim` of the target is below 2; GL_TEXTURE_DEPTH_EXT (0x8071) +0x24, minus twice the border when the dimension is below 3; GL_TEXTURE_INTERNAL_FORMAT (0x1003) the low 32 bits of +0x18; GL_TEXTURE_BORDER (0x1005) +0x28; and GL_TEXTURE_RED_SIZE, GREEN, BLUE, ALPHA, LUMINANCE and INTENSITY_SIZE (0x805c to 0x8061) the words +0x2c, +0x30, +0x34, +0x38, +0x3c and +0x40. The values are converted to a float.

- `__glOdyim_GetMaterialiv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd987ad0, 184 bytes): **established.**
  The integer form with the same colour-material test; the tracked case calls the get entry at +0x1f4 with GL_CURRENT_COLOR and the other cases the generic `__glim_GetMaterialiv`.
- `__glOdyim_GetFogFuncSGIS` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd987b90, 112 bytes): **established.**
  glGetFogFuncSGIS (argument: a float array). Inside glBegin and glEnd it raises 0x502; otherwise it copies the fog function's points, eight bytes per point and a point count taken from +0x16430, from +0x16434 into the array.

## Display-list parameters and execution thunks

- `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973450, 236 bytes): **established.**
  glListParameterfvSGIX (arguments: list name, parameter, a float array). Inside glBegin and glEnd it raises 0x502; a parameter other than GL_LIST_PRIORITY_SGIX (0x8182) raises 0x500; list name 0 raises 0x501. Otherwise it locks the list's record in the name table at +0x1340 with `__glNamesLockData`, stores the first float clamped to 0.0 to 1.0 at +0x2c of the record, and unlocks it; an unknown list name changes nothing.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameterfSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973540, 56 bytes): **established.**
  Puts its float argument in a stack slot and calls `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` with the slot's address.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameterivSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973580, 68 bytes): **established.**
  Converts the first integer of the array to a float and calls `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` with a pointer to it.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameteriSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9735d0, 64 bytes): **established.**
  Converts its integer argument to a float and calls `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` with a pointer to it.
- `__glOdyim_GetListParameterfvSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973610, 204 bytes): **established.**
  glGetListParameterfvSGIX (arguments: list name, parameter, a float array). The same 0x502, 0x500 and 0x501 checks as the setter; for a list found in the name table at +0x1340 it copies the float at +0x2c of the record into the array and unlocks the record.
- `__glOdyim_GetListParameterivSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9736e0, 92 bytes): **established.**
  Calls the float getter with a stack slot and stores the slot's value, converted to an integer, in the integer array.
- `__glOdysseyDlistOptimizer` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973740, 8 bytes): **established.**
  The list optimiser entry the board's context installs at +0x134c: a return with no instructions before it, so lists are not rewritten after compilation.
- `Bogus` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd974980, 8 bytes): **established.**
  A list-execution entry that does nothing and returns its argument (the pointer to its own record) unchanged, so a placeholder opcode advances nowhere.
- `callSetError` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd974c20, 68 bytes): **established.**
  Replays a recorded error when a list runs (argument: the record). It calls `__glSetError` with the word at +0 of the record and returns the address 4 bytes on.
- `__glOdyle_Begin` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9748f0, 68 bytes): **established.**
  Replays a recorded glBegin when a list runs (argument: the record). It calls the routine whose address is in the process word 0x106c with the primitive mode stored at +0 of the record and returns the address 4 bytes on.
- `__glOdyle_End` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd974940, 64 bytes): **established.**
  Replays a recorded glEnd: it calls the routine in the process word 0x107c and returns the record's own address, because the record has no operands.
- `__glsplc_AddSentinel` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd976070, 156 bytes): **established.**
  Ends a list that is being compiled. When the compile buffer (write pointer at the process word 0x1030, pending word count at 0x1034) holds pending words it writes their count in front of them and adds them with `__glsplc_AddIlist` using the push entry in +0x13b8; otherwise it backs the write pointer up 4 bytes (`addiu t0,a3,-4`, stored at 4144) and credits one word of space in 0x1028. It then adds `__glsple_Sentinel` as the closing instruction.
- `__glOdylc_Error` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`, 0xd974c70, 364 bytes): **established.**
  Compiles a glGetError-style raised error into the list being built (argument: the error code). It flushes any pending words as `__glsplc_AddIlist` does, reserves two words with `__glSPDlistSpace` when the free count at 0x1028 is empty, adds an instruction whose executor is `callSetError` and stores the code in its record.

Thirty-three routines of the compile path (the `__glOdysplc_` thunks and `__glOdyim_ArrayElementEXT`) are the same 17 instructions in 68 bytes (checked by comparing the listings with the callee and the numbers masked): they clear the byte at +0x169dc of the current context (the process word 0x1010) and then call the generic routine named in the last column. The table was produced by a script from the listings of `libglcore/asm/ODYSSEY/ody_dlist.s` and is **inferred** from that comparison, not from a separate reading of each body.

## o32 image-entry wrappers and array draws

These entries are from the o32 IP30 build at `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, not the N32 build described above. I read each complete body from its own o32 listing. The indirect calls are left as table offsets because this stripped build does not provide a reliable source-level name for those slots.

- `__glOdyle_TexImage1D` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd972a30): **established.**
  Occupies 344 bytes. It copies the 36-byte image-state record at the current context's +0x4a4 into a stack record, refreshes the same 36-byte record from the process-global pointer held in the routine's GOT slot, then passes the caller's words at +0 through +0x18 and a pointer to caller record +0x20 to the function pointer in the process table at +0x1100 (the load at 0xd972b10). It returns caller record +0x20 plus the caller's +0x1c length rounded up to four bytes; the final `lw`/`addiu` pair is at 0xd972b64/0xd972b84 in the exact o32 body.
- `__glOdyle_TexImage2D` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd972b88): **established.**
  Occupies 352 bytes. It performs the same two 36-byte state copies, passes caller words through +0x1c and a pointer to +0x24 to the function pointer in the process table at +0x1104 (the load at 0xd972c70), and returns +0x24 after rounding the caller's +0x20 length up to a four-byte boundary; the return arithmetic is at 0xd972cc4-0xd972ce4.
- `__glOdyle_TexImage3DEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd972ce8): **established.**
  Occupies 360 bytes. After the two 36-byte state copies, it forwards caller words through +0x20 and a pointer to +0x2c to the process-table function at +0x1360 (loaded at 0xd972dd8), then returns +0x2c plus the four-byte-rounded caller length at +0x24; the exact final calculation is at 0xd972e2c-0xd972e4c.
- `__glOdyle_TexSubImage1DEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd972e50): **established.**
  Occupies 336 bytes. It copies the same 36-byte context records, forwards caller words through +0x14 and a pointer to +0x1c to the process-table function at +0x12fc (the load at 0xd972f28), and returns +0x1c plus the four-byte-rounded caller length at +0x18; the return path is at 0xd972f7c-0xd972f9c.
- `__glOdyle_TexSubImage2DEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd972fa0): **established.**
  Occupies 352 bytes. It copies both 36-byte state records, forwards caller words through +0x1c and a pointer to +0x24 to the process-table function at +0x1300 (the load at 0xd973088), and returns +0x24 plus the four-byte-rounded caller length at +0x20; the exact return instructions are 0xd9730dc-0xd9730fc.
- `__glOdyle_TexSubImage3DEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd973100): **established.**
  Occupies 368 bytes. It copies both 36-byte state records, forwards caller words through +0x20 and a pointer to +0x2c to the process-table function at +0x1364 (the load at 0xd9731f8), and returns +0x2c plus the four-byte-rounded caller length at +0x28; the exact return instructions are 0xd97324c-0xd97326c.
- `__glOdysplc_DrawElements` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd9778f0): **established.**
  Occupies 72 bytes. It loads the current context from process word 0x1010, clears that context's byte at +0x169dc with `sb` at 0xd977914, calls the function pointer in its GOT at 0xd977918, and returns that call's result without changing its arguments.
- `__glOdysplc_DrawArraysEXT` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, 0xd977938): **established.**
  Occupies 72 bytes. Its complete body mirrors the preceding draw entry: it loads process word 0x1010, clears byte +0x169dc with `sb` at 0xd97795c, calls the separate GOT function pointer loaded at 0xd977960, and returns that call's result with the caller arguments unchanged.

## 64-bit IP30 library: ioctl, DMA and memory paths

These entries read `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` directly.

- `__glOdysseyIoctl` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x326da60
  **Established effects.** This 60-byte wrapper loads a word at argument 0 + 0x9e98 (`lw` at 0x326da88) and passes it as argument 0 to `ioctl`, with its own arguments 1 and 2 forwarded (`jalr` at 0x326da84). Its return is the result of that call (`jr` at 0x326da94).
- `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadowTest` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x326daa0
  **Established effects.** The 56-byte routine loads a doubleword from absolute address 0x1020 (`ld` at 0x326dac4) in the delay slot of its call to `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadow`, passing that value and zero as arguments 0 and 1 (`bgezall` at 0x326dac0). It returns the callee's result (`jr` at 0x326dad0); the role of the absolute address is Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadow` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x326dae0
  **Established effects.** This body occupies 100 bytes. It calls `__glOdysseyIoctl` with entry argument 0, request 0x6f64000c and entry argument 1 (`bgezall` at 0x326db08). When that result is negative, `bltz` at 0x326db10 is taken and the routine loads a doubleword at entry argument 0 + 0x28 (`ld` at 0x326db2c), then calls the resulting unknown target (`jalr` at 0x326db34). The routine's hardware role and callback effect are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseySwapBuffers` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x326db50
  **Established effects.** The 856-byte routine reads the byte at argument 0 + 0xde6 (`lbu` at 0x326db54); `beqz` at 0x326db74 is taken to 0x326ddd0 when it is zero and falls through otherwise. On the other path, word stores to absolute 0x10000 include 0x10443, 0xfa, 0x10046 and 0x10019 (`sw` at 0x326db84, 0x326db8c, 0x326db94, 0x326dba0); this is a partial list.
  It writes zero at argument 0 + 0xbb80 (`sw` at 0x326dbc4), reads a halfword at argument 0 + 0xca04 and stores a halfword of unknown value there (`lhu` at 0x326dbd8; `sh` at 0x326dbf0), and writes 0x10a5a and the word read from argument 0 + 0x19304 ORed with 0x8000 to absolute 0x10000 (`lw` at 0x326dbf4; `sw` at 0x326dbf8, 0x326dc00).
  Later it writes the word at argument 0 + 0xb9f4 ORed with 0x800000 to the same absolute address (`lw` at 0x326dce8; `sw` at 0x326dcf4). It calls `__glOdysseyIoctl` with entry argument 0, request 0x3ed and a stack address (`bgezall` at 0x326ddc4); the request includes a word from argument 0 + 0x9e9c and two zero words (`lw` at 0x326ddac; `sw` at 0x326ddc8, 0x326ddb0, 0x326ddbc). If that call returns negative, `bltz` at 0x326ddcc takes the path to an unknown indirect callee (`jalr` at 0x326de98). The role of the routine and effects of the command words and callback are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyFlush` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x326deb0
  **Established effects.** This 96-byte body writes words to absolute address 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x326deb8). In order, the values are 0x10443 (`sw` at 0x326deb8); 0xfa (`sw` at 0x326dec0), two 0x10046 (`sw` at 0x326dec8, 0x326decc); 0x10019 (`sw` at 0x326ded4); 0x10443 (`sw` at 0x326ded8); 0x96 (`sw` at 0x326dee0), and four 0x10046 (`sw` at 0x326dee4, 0x326dee8, 0x326deec, 0x326def0). It writes zero to argument 0 + 0xbb80 (`sw` at 0x326def8), then writes 0x10443, 0xfa and two 0x10046 to 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x326defc, 0x326df00, 0x326df04, 0x326df0c). The routine's role and the destination's hardware role are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyFinish` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 804 bytes
  **Established effects.**
  - The initial word stores to process address 0x10000 include 0x10443, 0xfa, two copies of 0x10046, and 0x10019 (`sw` at 0x326df18, 0x326df20, 0x326df28, 0x326df2c, 0x326df34). It also clears the word at argument 0 + 0xbb80 (`sw` at 0x326df94).
  - It reads the halfword at argument 0 + 0xca04 (`lhu` at 0x326e110). A register is incremented by 1 (`addiu` at 0x326e114), and a halfword of unknown value is stored at the same argument offset (`sh` at 0x326e11c). The routine then writes 0x10a5a, the word from argument 0 + 0x19304, and a third value that hostfacts leaves unknown to 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x326e12c, 0x326e130, 0x326e134; `lw` at 0x326e124).
  - A repeated halfword read uses an unknown address (`lhu` at 0x326e178); `bne` at 0x326e17c loops when the compared values differ and falls through when equal. The read’s purpose and the routine’s overall role remain unknown.
- `__glOdysseyDriverMakeCurrent` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 604 bytes
  **Established effects.**
  - The word at argument 0 + 0xe34 is read (`lw` at 0x326e430). `blez` at 0x326e44c branches when it is signed nonpositive; when it falls through, 2 is stored in the stack record (`sw` at 0x326e458).
  - The routine calls `__glOdysseyIoctl` with argument 0, 0x6f640008, a stack address, and either 0 or 2 as the fourth argument (`bgezall` at 0x326e498). `bltz` at 0x326e4a0 takes the path to an indirect call if the returned value is signed negative and falls through if it is nonnegative.
  - The negative-result path calls an unknown callee (`jalr` at 0x326e65c); the purpose of that call and the routine’s overall role are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyImageXfer` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 956 bytes
  **Established effects.**
  - Calls include `__glOdysseyImageSaveUserBuzzRasterState` (`jalr` at 0x321d0b4), `__glOdysseyImageLoadBuzzRasterState` with first argument 0 (`jalr` at 0x321d0c4), and `__glOdysseyImageChainCreate` with the original first four arguments (`bgezall` at 0x321d0dc).
  - `beqz` at 0x321d0e4 takes an alternate path when the chain creation result is zero and falls through when nonzero. On fall-through, `__glOdysseyImageTilingCreate` receives the original third argument, the chain result, and the original fifth argument (`jalr` at 0x321d0fc); `beqz` at 0x321d104 branches on a zero result and falls through on a nonzero result.
  - An indirect call has an unknown callee and receives a stack address as its second argument (`jalr` at 0x321d1cc). `beqz` at 0x321d1dc takes the path calling `__glSetError` with 0x505 when the low byte of that result is zero, and falls through when nonzero (`jalr` at 0x321d330). The indirect callee’s purpose and the routine’s overall role are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyImageMovePixelsDMAtoGfx` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 6932 bytes
  **Established effects.**
  - The routine calls `__glOdysseyOpenPMObjWriteReference`; its first argument is the doubleword read at process address 0x1020, the next two are words at original argument 0 + 0x10 and + 0x14, and the fourth is a stack address (`ld` at 0x324e120; `lw` at 0x324e600, 0x324e5d4; `jalr` at 0x324e61c). `beqz` at 0x324e628 takes a separate path when the low result byte is zero and falls through otherwise.
  - `beqz` at 0x324e994 skips a group of stores when the word at +0xbb80 of the doubleword loaded from 0x1020 is zero and falls through when it is nonzero (`lw` at 0x324e948). On fall-through, it writes 0x10443, 0xfa and two copies of 0x10046 to process address 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x324e9a4, 0x324e9ac, 0x324e9b4, 0x324e9b8). A later store writes 0x11453 to that address (`sw` at 0x324e9e4).
  - Calls also include `__glOdysseyClosePMObjWriteReference` (`jalr` at 0x324f060) and `__glOdysseyIoctl` with first argument the doubleword read at 0x1020, second argument 0x6f64000d and a stack address as the third (`jalr` at 0x324fbf4). The interpretation of these values and the routine’s overall role remain unknown.
- `__glOdysseyImageMovePixelsDMAtoHost` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1892 bytes
  **Established effects.**
  - Calls include `__glOdysseyImageCalcXferReadParams`, with the words at argument 0 + 0x8 and + 0xc as its first two arguments, the original second argument as its third and a stack address as its fourth (`lw` at 0x324fcb8, 0x324fcac; `bgezall` at 0x324fd14).
  - `__glOdysseyOpenPMObjReference` is called with the doubleword loaded at process address 0x1020 as its first argument and the words at original argument 1 + 0x10 and + 0x14 as the next two (`ld` at 0x324fc30; `lw` at 0x324ff68, 0x324ff3c; `jalr` at 0x324ff64). `beqz` at 0x324ff70 takes a separate path when the low byte of its result is zero and falls through otherwise.
  - The routine calls `__glOdysseyReadFromPMObj` (`jalr` at 0x3250284). `beqz` at 0x3250290 takes a separate path when the low result byte is zero; when it falls through, the routine calls `__glOdysseyClosePMObjReference` (`jalr` at 0x32502a8). The object reference’s hardware effect and the routine’s overall role are unknown.
- `ImageCalcDmaFieldOffsets` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 604 bytes

  **Established effects.** When entry `a0` is zero, `beqz` at 0x3252f20 selects tests of entry `a1`. Values 1, 3 and 4 each take their respective `beq` branches to write zero to the words at entry `a4` and `a5` (`beq` at 0x3252f80; `beq` at 0x3252f88; `beq` at 0x3252f90; `sw` at 0x3253014; `sw` at 0x325301c). Another path calls `__glSetError` with argument 0x502 (`jalr` at 0x3252f9c). The output words and the routine's broader role remain Unknown.
- `ImageToDmaHostPixelSize` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 24 bytes

  **Established effects.** The routine shifts entry `a0` left two bits (`dsll` at 0x32535c4), adds it to a table base (`daddu` at 0x32535cc), and loads the result word from table 0x33881e0 in the return delay slot (`lw` at 0x32535d4; `jr` at 0x32535d0). The table contents and the routine's broader role remain Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyOpenPMObjReference` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 600 bytes

  **Established effects.** Entry `a1` equal to -3 or -4 takes separate special paths (`beq` at 0x325b7a0; `beq` at 0x325b7bc). Other values call `LookupKnownGroup` with third argument 1 (`bgezall` at 0x325b7dc). The `beqz` at 0x325b7e4 skips `LookupCoresidRange` when the first call returns zero; otherwise the second call receives 1 as its fifth argument (`bgezall` at 0x325b804). When the returned range's word at +0x18 is zero (`lw` at 0x325b80c), `beqz` at 0x325b810 takes a path calling `OpenPipeMemReference` with that range as its third argument and 0 as its fourth (`bgezall` at 0x325b930).

  In the -3 path, `bnez` at 0x325b880 skips the following sequence when entry `a5` is nonzero. Otherwise the stores to 0x10000 include 0x10443 and 0xfa (`sw` at 0x325b890; `sw` at 0x325b898); the routine also clears entry `a0 + 0xbb80` (`sw` at 0x325b8d0). The routine role and destination role are Unknown.

  **Established effects.** `beqz` at 0x32487c4 branches when `s0` is zero and falls through otherwise; hostfacts expresses `s0` as `a1`. The next `beq` at 0x32487d0 branches when `s0` equals 1 and falls through otherwise.

- `__glOdysseyProgramFFTFilter` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 656 bytes
  **Established effects.** A doubleword is loaded from argument 0 + 0x20 (`ld` at 0x32138dc). `beq` at 0x32138f8 branches when `a1` equals 1 and falls through otherwise.
  - On fall-through, calls to `__glOdysseyInitLerpTree` include calls with the loaded pointer + 0x5a0 and + 0x870 as first arguments (`jalr` at 0x3213904, 0x3213914). The broader role of this call sequence and the routine is unknown.
- `__glOdysseyImageLoadBuzzRasterState` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 440 bytes
  **Established effects.** A doubleword is read from process address 0x1020 (`ld` at 0x32563c0). One copied word comes from either +0x13f78 or +0x1404c relative to that pointer (`lw` at 0x32563e0) and is stored at +0xa000 (`sw` at 0x32563e4).
  - A loop reads two words and separately stores two words, with the load bases, store bases and stored values unknown (`lw` at 0x32564bc; `sw` at 0x32564c8; `lw` at 0x32564d0; `sw` at 0x32564dc); `bgtz` at 0x32564d8 repeats when the tested value is signed positive and falls through otherwise. It ends with a word store of 2 to process address 0x1030 (`sw` at 0x3256574). The meaning of the copied state and the routine’s broader role are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyMapSepFilterToAlphas` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1600 bytes
  **Established effects.** `beq` at 0x3247acc branches when `a3` equals 2 and falls through otherwise. On fall-through, `beq` at 0x3247ad8 branches when `a3` equals 3 and falls through otherwise.
  - One path calls `__glOdysseyProgramConvolutionFilter` with an unknown first argument, original argument 0 as its second, the symbolic expression `(a2 + a3)` as its third, and original argument 4 as its fourth (`bgezall` at 0x3247bcc). The calculation’s meaning and the routine’s broader role are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyScaleBiasFilter` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 340 bytes
  **Established effects.** It reads floating-point words from argument 0, including offsets +0x2c and +0x18 (`lwc1` at 0x3242000, 0x3242014). `blez` at 0x3242028 takes the return path when `a1` is signed nonpositive and falls through when it is positive.
  - One loop reads floating-point words from unknown addresses (`lwc1` at 0x3242038, 0x324203c, 0x3242040, 0x3242048), executes multiply-add instructions (`madd.s` at 0x3242044, 0x324204c, 0x3242054), and writes floating-point words to unknown addresses (`swc1` at 0x324205c, 0x3242064, 0x3242068, 0x3242074). The values and addresses are Unknown. `bnez` at 0x3242070 repeats when its tested value is nonzero and falls through when it is zero.
  - A second loop likewise reads and writes floating-point words at unknown addresses (`lwc1` at 0x3242084; `swc1` at 0x32420b8). `bne` at 0x3242144 repeats while the compared values differ and falls through when they are equal. The buffer identities, computed values and broader role of the routine remain unknown.

## 64-bit IP30 library: context and program paths

- `__glOdysseyClear` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x31f8e30
  **Established effects.** This 2008-byte body reads argument 0 + 0xbb80 (`lw` at 0x31f9024); when that word is nonzero, `beqz` at 0x31f904c falls through to clear it (`sw` at 0x31f9054) and write 0x10443, 0xfa and two 0x10046 words to absolute 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x31f905c, 0x31f9064, 0x31f906c, 0x31f9070). It next writes 0x10420 and the word at argument 0 + 0x9f80 to 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x31f907c; `lw` at 0x31f9080; `sw` at 0x31f9084).
  One path calls `__glOdysseyIoctl` with request 0x6f64000e and a stack address (`jalr` at 0x31f92dc); `beqz` at 0x31f92ec is taken if the result is zero. Another path writes 0x14400, 7, 0x8f80a944 and 0x14001 to absolute 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x31f9358, 0x31f9360, 0x31f9370, 0x31f93d4). These command lists are partial; their hardware effects and the routine’s broader role are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidateHardware` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x31fc4a0
  **Established effects.** The body occupies 5028 bytes. It reads argument 0 + 0xbb80 (`lw` at 0x31fc4a8); if nonzero, `beqz` at 0x31fc4b4 falls through to clear that word (`sw` at 0x31fc4bc) and write 0x10443, 0xfa and two 0x10046 words to absolute 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x31fc4c4, 0x31fc4cc, 0x31fc4d4, 0x31fc4d8).
  It reads argument 0 + 0x18e54 (`lw` at 0x31fc4b8); `beqz` at 0x31fc4e0 falls through when bit 1 is set. That path writes 0x10404 and the word at argument 0 + 0x9f4c to 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x31fc520; `lw` at 0x31fc524; `sw` at 0x31fc528). The roles of the routine, these words and the destination are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyAsyncDropMarker` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 0x31ea390
  **Established effects.** The 500-byte body reads the word at argument 0 + 0x820 (`lw` at 0x31ea390) and combines it with argument 1 (`and` at 0x31ea39c). In the path reached by `beq` at 0x31ea3a8, the compared value is unknown and equals 2; the code decrements the word at argument 0 + 0x285c4 (`lw` at 0x31ea440; `sw` at 0x31ea44c) and reads argument 0 + 0x285d0 (`lwu` at 0x31ea444).
  At the later `beqz` at 0x31ea450, the tested value is the word at argument 0 + 0x285d0: zero takes the branch, and nonzero falls through. Its delay slot clears that word on either path (`sw` at 0x31ea454). Later stores include a doubleword to an unknown destination (`sd` at 0x31ea4c8). The roles of the routine, field and destination are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyProgramTexFilter` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 572 bytes
  - **Established effects.** The word at process address 0x1030 is read (`lw` at 0x324aa1c). `beq` at 0x324aa3c branches when it equals 1 and falls through otherwise; the taken path calls `__glSetError` with first argument 0x502 (`jalr` at 0x324ab60).
  - On the fall-through path, calls to `ConvertToLerps` include calls with original argument 0 and with argument 0 + 0x94 as their first arguments (`bgezall` at 0x324aa54, 0x324aa60). It also calls `PackLerpTree` (`bgezall` at 0x324aa8c). The routine's broader role and the resulting fields' meanings are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyProgramConvolutionFilter` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1296 bytes
  - **Established effects.** The word at process address 0x1030 is read (`lw` at 0x324ac60). `beq` at 0x324ac80 branches when it equals 1 and falls through otherwise; the taken path calls `__glSetError` with first argument 0x502 (`jalr` at 0x324b140).
  - Calls include `ConvertToLerps` with the original first four arguments (`bgezall` at 0x324acd8), `__glOdysseyProgram2DOffsets` with the original first and third arguments and an unknown second argument (`bgezall` at 0x324ad30), and `__glOdysseyProgram1DOffsets` with the same known argument positions (`bgezall` at 0x324b128). The broader routine role, the effects of these calls on the board and the resulting fields' meanings are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyProgramGenericConvolutionFilter` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1136 bytes
  - **Established effects.** The routine reads the word at process address 0x1030 (`lw` at 0x324b174). `beq` at 0x324b194 takes the path calling `__glSetError` with first argument 0x502 when the word equals 1, and falls through otherwise (`jalr` at 0x324b5c0).
  - When `beq` at 0x324b1c4 falls through because the compared word differs from 0x1102, it calls `ConvertToLerps` with a calculated first argument and the original second argument (`bgezall` at 0x324b1d4), then calls `PackLerpTree` with the original first argument (`bgezall` at 0x324b1e4). The routine's broader role and how the calculated values are used remain unknown.
- `__glOdysseyProgram1DOffsets` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 616 bytes
  - **Established effects.** `beqz` at 0x3248108 branches when argument 1 is zero and falls through when nonzero; on fall-through, `beq` at 0x3248110 branches when argument 1 equals 1 and falls through otherwise.
  - In one path, `beq` at 0x324819c is taken when its unknown tested value equals 1 and falls through when it does not. The taken path stores zero words at argument 0 + 0xb50, +0xb4c, +0xb44, +0xb48 and +0xb40 (`sw` at 0x3248244, 0x3248248, 0x324824c, 0x3248250, 0x3248258). The routine's overall role and the five fields' meanings are unknown.
- `__glOdysseyProgram2DOffsets` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1104 bytes
  - **Established effects.** `beqz` at 0x324834c takes the direct path when argument 1 is at least 0xe as an unsigned value and falls through when it is less. On the direct path, it stores zero at argument 0 + 0xb50, -1 at +0xb4c, 1 at +0xb44, -1 at +0xb48 and -1 at +0xb40 (`sw` at 0x3248360, 0x3248368, 0x3248370, 0x3248374, 0x324837c). The broader role of this routine and the meanings of the stored fields are unknown.
  - The fall-through path jumps through a table (`jr` at 0x3248358); the table includes entries leading to the same store sequence and to other paths.
- `__glOdysseyInitKernel` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 656 bytes

  **Established effects.** The routine calls `__glOdysseyIoctl` with entry `a0`, request 0x6f640000, and entry `sp - 0x80` (`jalr` at 0x325aa48). A zero result takes `beqz` at 0x325aa50 to a path that reads a word and a doubleword from the stack (`lw` at 0x325aa78; `ld` at 0x325aa80). That path also stores a word and a doubleword at unknown bases; hostfacts leaves both stored values unknown (`sw` at 0x325aa7c; `sd` at 0x325aa84). Another path calls an unknown callee with entry `a0` and 0x428 (`jalr` at 0x325ab68); `beqz` at 0x325ab74 takes the zero-result path, calling `__glSetError` with 0x505 (`jalr` at 0x325abc0). The purpose of those stores and the routine’s broader role are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidate` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 152 bytes

  **Established effects.** The word at entry `a0 + 0x18e58` controls the first call (`lw` at 0x31fc0c0): when it is nonzero, `beqz` at 0x31fc0dc falls through to `__glOdysseyValidateContext` (`bgezall` at 0x31fc0e8). A nonzero word at `a0 + 0x4714` makes `beqz` at 0x31fc0f4 fall through to an indirect call with unknown callee (`lw` at 0x31fc0f0; `jalr` at 0x31fc100). Another tested word is unknown; `beqz` at 0x31fc110 skips `__glOdysseyValidateHardware` when it is zero (`bgezall` at 0x31fc118). The routine finishes with byte clears to unknown bases, including the return delay slot (`sb` at 0x31fc12c; `jr` at 0x31fc140; `sb` at 0x31fc144). The role of those clears and the routine’s broader role are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidateContext` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 716 bytes

  **Established effects.** On one path, `beqz` at 0x31fdbdc falls through when bit 0x200 of the tested value is set and calls `__glOdysseyValidateTextureState` (`jalr` at 0x31fdbe8). Later, `beqz` at 0x31fdc68 skips `__glOdysseyValidateClipPlanes` when its tested register is zero (`bgezall` at 0x31fdc70). If it falls through, the routine calls `__glOdysseyGetNumSWClipPlanes` (`jalr` at 0x31fdc7c); a signed nonpositive result takes `blez` at 0x31fdc84 past the `__glOdysseyValidateSoftClipPlanes` call (`jalr` at 0x31fdc90). The tested values and the routine’s broader role remain Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidateClipPlanes` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 844 bytes

  **Established effects.** One path writes words to 0x10000, including 0x908091bb (`sw` at 0x31fc214), followed by values loaded from entry `a0 + 0xc004` and `a0 + 0xc010` (`lwc1` at 0x31fc218; `swc1` at 0x31fc21c; `lwc1` at 0x31fc230; `swc1` at 0x31fc240). Other stores occur between those two values (`swc1` at 0x31fc224, 0x31fc22c). The routine later calls `__glOdysseyGetNumSWClipPlanes` (`jalr` at 0x31fc3e0); a nonzero result makes `beqz` at 0x31fc3f0 fall through, and the routine calls `__glOdysseyGetNumHWClipPlanes` (`jalr` at 0x31fc404). The 0x10000 destination's role and the routine’s broader role are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidateContextRasterMisc` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 496 bytes

  **Established effects.** It tests bit 1 of the word at entry `a0 + 0x808` (`lw` at 0x31fd850; `andi` at 0x31fd85c); when clear, `beqz` at 0x31fd868 takes a branch to the common update. The code stores an unknown word to `a0 + 0x9f4c` (`sw` at 0x31fd8a8), sets bit 1 of `a0 + 0x18e54` (`lw` at 0x31fd8ac; `ori` at 0x31fd8b0; `sw` at 0x31fd8b4), and writes 2 to 0x1030 (`sw` at 0x31fd8bc). The fields and the routine’s broader role remain Unknown.
- `__glOdysseySetProgram` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 1240 bytes

  **Established effects.** The byte at entry `a0 + 0x285e0` determines the first path (`lbu` at 0x325e4a0). When it is nonzero, `beqz` at 0x325e4b4 falls through to a return whose delay slot writes 0x298080c5 to 0x10000 (`jr` at 0x325e4bc; `sw` at 0x325e4c0). When it is zero, `beqz` at 0x325e4d8 returns if the word at `a0 + 0xc9a4` is zero (`lw` at 0x325e4cc).

  Another path reads a halfword from an unknown address (`lhu` at 0x325e54c) and stores a word of unknown value to 0x10000 (`sw` at 0x325e564). Later stores to 0x10000 include 0x90808800 and two zero words (`sw` at 0x325e8a8; `sw` at 0x325e8ac; `sw` at 0x325e8b0). The routine's broader role and the effect of those writes are Unknown.
- `__glOdysseyValidateProgram` — `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` — 332 bytes

  **Established effects.** A nonzero byte at entry `a0 + 0x285e0` makes `beqz` at 0x325e36c fall through to a store of 0x298080c5 at 0x10000 (`lbu` at 0x325e350; `sw` at 0x325e380), followed by return (`jr` at 0x325e388). If the byte is zero, the branch instead takes a path that clears words at `a0 + 0xc9f8` and `a0 + 0xc9a4` (`sw` at 0x325e3a4; `sw` at 0x325e3b0), calls `AddProgram` with second argument 0 (`bgezall` at 0x325e3b8), then calls `__glOdysseyGetNumSWClipPlanes` (`jalr` at 0x325e3c4). A nonzero result makes `beqz` at 0x325e3cc fall through to another `AddProgram` call with second argument 1 (`bgezall` at 0x325e3e0).

  If the word at `a0 + 0xc9a4` is nonzero, `beqz` at 0x325e410 falls through to a loop with two word stores to 0x10000 (`lw` at 0x325e3f0; `sw` at 0x325e434; `sw` at 0x325e450); `bnez` at 0x325e460 repeats when the unknown value in `a2` is unsigned less than the word read from `a0 + 0xc9a4`; it falls through otherwise (`lw` at 0x325e454). The effect of those writes and the routine's broader role are Unknown.

If nonzero, `beqz` at 0x31fdeac falls through and writes 0, 2, 10 and 14 to argument 0 + 0xc52c, +0xc530, +0xc534 and +0xc538 respectively (`sw` at 0x31fdeb4, 0x31fdeb8, 0x31fdebc, 0x31fdec4).
